Implement the following plan:

# Issue #8: Download Auswertung — Server-seitiges PDF

## Context
"Download Auswertung komplett" nutzt `html-to-image` (`toPng()`), das den gesamten DOM klont — inkl. SVG-Karte, Chart.js-Diagramme, große Tabellen. Bei umfangreichen Auswertungen crasht der Browser (500MB+ RAM, Main Thread blockiert). Lösung: PDF-Generierung auf dem Server mit PDFKit (pure JS, kein Chromium nötig, läuft auf Alpine).

**Scope:** Nur die "Download Auswertung komplett"-Buttons auf beiden Seiten werden auf PDF umgestellt. Einzelne Chart-Downloads (Karte, Balken, Torte) bleiben als PNG via html-to-image.

## Ansatz: PDFKit (pure JavaScript)

- Kein Chromium/Puppeteer nötig → kein Dockerfile-Umbau
- SVG-Pfade der Austria-Karte können direkt mit PDFKit gezeichnet werden
- Bar-/Pie-Charts werden mit PDFKit-Zeichenprimitiven (Rechtecke/Kreissegmente) nachgebaut
- Tabellen als strukturierter Text

## Änderungen

### 1. Dependency hinzufügen
```
cd server && npm install pdfkit @types/pdfkit
```

### 2. Neue Datei: `server/src/services/pdf.ts`

Shared PDF-Generator mit Helper-Funktionen:

- `renderHeader(doc, title, subtitle)` — BOS-ARSA Branding (dunkelblaue Leiste, Titel, Slogan)
- `renderSummaryCards(doc, stats, exerciseCount?)` — Teilnehmer/Rapporte/Übungen Zahlen
- `renderPerRepeater(doc, perRepeater)` — Repeater-Übersicht Grid
- `renderExerciseTable(doc, exercises)` — Übungsliste (nur aggregiert)
- `renderAustriaMap(doc, blStats)` — SVG-Pfade aus AustriaMap.tsx, Farben nach Teilnehmerzahl
- `renderBarChart(doc, chartData)` — Balkendiagramm mit Rechtecken
- `renderPieChart(doc, chartData)` — Tortendiagramm mit Kreissegmenten
- `renderBezirkTable(doc, bezirkStats)` — Bezirk/BL Tabelle mit Hierarchie
- `renderBlTable(doc, blStats)` — Bundesland-Tabelle (Fallback ohne Bezirke)
- `renderParticipants(doc, participants)` — Teilnehmer-Liste
- `renderFooter(doc)` — "oeradio.at · bosarsa.oeradio.at"

Austria-Karte: SVG-Pfaddaten aus `AustriaMap.tsx` extrahieren und als Konstante in pdf.ts ablegen. PDFKit zeichnet die Pfade mit `doc.path(d).fill(color)`. Farblogik (Heatmap nach Teilnehmern) wird 1:1 übernommen.

### 3. Neue Datei: `server/src/routes/pdf.ts`

Zwei Endpunkte:

**GET `/api/v1/pdf/reports?from=...&to=...&types=...`** — Aggregierte Auswertung
- Nutzt gleiche DB-Queries wie `reports.ts:/stats`
- Ruft PDF-Generator auf mit exercises + stats
- Response: `application/pdf` mit Content-Disposition

**GET `/api/v1/pdf/exercises/:id`** — Einzel-Übung Auswertung
- Nutzt gleiche DB-Queries wie `exercises.ts:/:id/stats`
- Ruft PDF-Generator auf mit exercise + stats
- Response: `application/pdf` mit Content-Disposition

### 4. Route registrieren: `server/src/index.ts`

```ts
import { pdfRouter } from './routes/pdf.js';
app.use('/api/v1/pdf', authMiddleware, pdfRouter);
```

### 5. Client: `AggregatedReportsPage.tsx`

- `downloadFullPage()` ersetzen: statt `toPng()` → `fetch('/api/v1/pdf/reports?from=...&to=...&types=...')` + Blob-Download
- Dateiname: `BOS-ARSA_Auswertung_{from}_{to}.pdf`
- `toPng`-Import kann bleiben (wird für einzelne Chart-Downloads weiter genutzt)

### 6. Client: `ReportsPage.tsx`

- `downloadFullPage()` ersetzen: statt `toPng()` → `fetch('/api/v1/pdf/exercises/${id}')` + Blob-Download
- Dateiname: `BOS-ARSA_Auswertung_{date}.pdf`

## PDF-Layout (A4 Querformat)

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ ██ BOS-ARSA Log  ·  Im Sinne der Sicherheit    │  Header
│    Auswertung 01.01.2026 – 31.03.2026          │
├─────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│  [42 Teiln.]  [128 Rapp.]  [12 Übungen]  [Ø 3] │  Summary
├─────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│  Schöckl 2m: 35  |  Lachtal: 28  |  OE-Link: 65│  Per-Repeater
├─────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│  Übungsliste (Datum | Typ | Name | Teiln | Rap) │  Exercises
├──────────────────────┬──────────────────────────┤
│  [Austria Map SVG]   │  [Bar Chart]             │  Visuals
│                      │  [Pie Chart]             │
├──────────────────────┴──────────────────────────┤
│  Bundesland/Bezirk | Teilnehmer | Rapporte      │  Table
├─────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│  # | Rufzeichen | Name | Bezirk | BL | Rapp.   │  Participants
├─────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│  oeradio.at · bosarsa.oeradio.at                │  Footer
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

## Dateien

| Datei | Änderung |
|-------|----------|
| `server/package.json` | `pdfkit` + `@types/pdfkit` hinzufügen |
| `server/src/services/pdf.ts` | **NEU** — PDF-Generator mit allen Render-Funktionen |
| `server/src/routes/pdf.ts` | **NEU** — API-Endpunkte für PDF-Download |
| `server/src/index.ts` | PDF-Route registrieren |
| `client/src/pages/AggregatedReportsPage.tsx` | `downloadFullPage` → Server-PDF-Fetch |
| `client/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx` | `downloadFullPage` → Server-PDF-Fetch |

## Verifikation
1. `nix-shell -p nodejs --run "npm run build"` kompiliert sauber
2. Aggregierte Auswertung: "Download Auswertung" → PDF wird sofort heruntergeladen, kein Browser-Freeze
3. Einzel-Übung: "Download Auswertung" → PDF mit Übungsdaten
4. Einzelne Chart-Downloads (Karte, Balken, Torte PNG) funktionieren weiterhin
5. PDF enthält: Header, Summary, Karte, Charts, Tabellen, Teilnehmer, Footer
6. Deploy mit `./deploy-production.sh`

     1→import { Router } from 'express';
     2→import { getDb } from '../db/database.js';
     3→
     4→export const reportsRouter = Router();
     5→
     6→// Aggregated stats across a date range
     7→reportsRouter.get('/stats', (req, res) => {
     8→  const { from, to, types } = req.query;
     9→  if (!from || !to) {
    10→    res.status(400).json({ error: 'from und to Parameter erforderlich (YYYY-MM-DD)' });
    11→    return;
    12→  }
    13→
    14→  const db = getDb();
    15→
    16→  // Fetch exercises in date range, optionally filtered by type
    17→  let typeFilter = '';
    18→  const queryParams: any[] = [from, to];
    19→  if (types && typeof types === 'string' && types.trim()) {
    20→    const typeList = types.split(',').map(t => t.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    21→    if (typeList.length > 0) {
    22→      typeFilter = ` AND e.exercise_type IN (${typeList.map(() => '?').join(',')})`;
    23→      queryParams.push(...typeList);
    24→    }
    25→  }
    26→
    27→  const exercises = db.prepare(
    28→    `SELECT e.id, e.date, e.name, e.oe_link_enabled, e.exercise_type,
    29→      (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_op_marker = 0) as participant_count,
    30→      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_op_marker = 0 AND readability IS NOT NULL) as report_count
    31→    FROM exercises e
    32→    WHERE e.date >= ? AND e.date <= ?${typeFilter}
    33→    ORDER BY e.date ASC`
    34→  ).all(...queryParams) as any[];
    35→
    36→  if (exercises.length === 0) {
    37→    res.json({
    38→      exercises: [],
    39→      stats: { totalParticipants: 0, totalReports: 0, perRepeater: [], bezirkStats: [], blStats: [], participants: [] },
    40→    });
    41→    return;
    42→  }
    43→
    44→  const ids = exercises.map((e: any) => e.id);
    45→  const placeholders = ids.map(() => '?').join(',');
    46→
    47→  const totalParticipants = (db.prepare(
    48→    `SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) as c FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND is_op_marker = 0`
    49→  ).get(...ids) as any)?.c || 0;
    50→
    51→  const totalReports = (db.prepare(
    52→    `SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND is_op_marker = 0 AND readability IS NOT NULL`
    53→  ).get(...ids) as any)?.c || 0;
    54→
    55→  const perRepeater = db.prepare(
    56→    `SELECT r.short_name, COUNT(*) as count
    57→    FROM signal_reports sr
    58→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
    59→    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND sr.is_op_marker = 0 AND sr.readability IS NOT NULL
    60→    GROUP BY sr.repeater_id
    61→    ORDER BY count DESC`
    62→  ).all(...ids);
    63→
    64→  const bezirkStats = db.prepare(
    65→    `SELECT bl.name as bundesland, bl.code as bundesland_code, bl.sort_order as bl_sort,
    66→      bz.code as bezirk_code, bz.name as bezirk_name, bz.is_capital,
    67→      COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,
    68→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports
    69→    FROM signal_reports sr
    70→    JOIN bezirke bz ON bz.code = sr.bezirk_code
    71→    JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = bz.bundesland_code
    72→    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
    73→    GROUP BY bz.code
    74→    ORDER BY bl.sort_order, bz.code`
    75→  ).all(...ids);
    76→
    77→  const blStats = db.prepare(
    78→    `SELECT
    79→      COALESCE(bl.name, 'Sonstige') as bundesland,
    80→      o.bundesland_code,
    81→      COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,
    82→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports
    83→    FROM signal_reports sr
    84→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
    85→    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
    86→    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
    87→    GROUP BY o.bundesland_code
    88→    ORDER BY o.bundesland_code`
    89→  ).all(...ids);
    90→
    91→  const participants = db.prepare(
    92→    `SELECT o.callsign, o.name, o.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code,
    93→      bl.name as bundesland_name,
    94→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as report_count
    95→    FROM signal_reports sr
    96→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
    97→    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
    98→    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
    99→    GROUP BY sr.operator_id
   100→    ORDER BY bl.sort_order, o.bezirk_code, o.callsign`
   101→  ).all(...ids);
   102→
   103→  res.json({
   104→    exercises,
   105→    stats: { totalParticipants, totalReports, perRepeater, bezirkStats, blStats, participants },
   106→  });
   107→});
   108→
   109→// ADIF export across a date range
   110→reportsRouter.get('/adif', (req, res) => {
   111→  const { from, to } = req.query;
   112→  if (!from || !to) {
   113→    res.status(400).json({ error: 'from und to Parameter erforderlich (YYYY-MM-DD)' });
   114→    return;
   115→  }
   116→
   117→  const db = getDb();
   118→
   119→  const exercises = db.prepare(
   120→    `SELECT id, date FROM exercises WHERE date >= ? AND date <= ? ORDER BY date ASC`
   121→  ).all(from, to) as { id: string; date: string }[];
   122→
   123→  if (exercises.length === 0) {
   124→    res.status(404).json({ error: 'Keine Übungen im gewählten Zeitraum' });
   125→    return;
   126→  }
   127→
   128→  const ids = exercises.map(e => e.id);
   129→  const placeholders = ids.map(() => '?').join(',');
   130→
   131→  const reports = db.prepare(
   132→    `SELECT sr.readability, sr.strength, sr.db_over_s9, sr.notes, sr.created_at,
   133→      o.callsign, o.name as op_name, o.qth,
   134→      r.short_name, r.frequency_mhz, r.band, r.type,
   135→      e.date as exercise_date
   136→    FROM signal_reports sr
   137→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
   138→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
   139→    JOIN exercises e ON e.id = sr.exercise_id
   140→    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders})
   141→      AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
   142→      AND sr.readability IS NOT NULL
   143→    ORDER BY e.date, sr.created_at`
   144→  ).all(...ids) as any[];
   145→
   146→  // Build ADIF
   147→  const adifField = (name: string, value: string) => {
   148→    if (!value) return '';
   149→    return `<${name}:${value.length}>${value} `;
   150→  };
   151→
   152→  let adif = '';
   153→
   154→  // Header
   155→  adif += 'ADIF Export from BOS-ARSA Log\n';
   156→  adif += `https://bosarsalog.oeradio.at/\n`;
   157→  adif += `Date range: ${from} to ${to}\n`;
   158→  adif += `QSO count: ${reports.length}\n`;
   159→  adif += '\n';
   160→  adif += adifField('ADIF_VER', '3.1.4');
   161→  adif += adifField('PROGRAMID', 'BOS-ARSA Log');
   162→  adif += adifField('PROGRAMVERSION', '1.0.5');
   163→  adif += '\n<EOH>\n\n';
   164→
   165→  // Records
   166→  for (const r of reports) {
   167→    const qsoDate = r.exercise_date.replace(/-/g, '');
   168→
   169→    // Extract time from created_at (format: "2026-02-16 14:30:00")
   170→    let timeOn = '0900'; // default
   171→    if (r.created_at) {
   172→      const timePart = r.created_at.split(' ')[1];
   173→      if (timePart) {
   174→        timeOn = timePart.replace(/:/g, '').slice(0, 4);
   175→      }
   176→    }
   177→
   178→    // RST: combine readability + strength + optional dB
   179→    const rst = `${r.readability}${r.strength}`;
   180→
   181→    // Frequency in MHz as string
   182→    const freq = r.frequency_mhz ? String(r.frequency_mhz) : '';
   183→
   184→    // Band mapping
   185→    let band = r.band || '';
   186→    if (!band && r.frequency_mhz) {
   187→      if (r.frequency_mhz >= 144 && r.frequency_mhz < 148) band = '2m';
   188→      else if (r.frequency_mhz >= 430 && r.frequency_mhz < 440) band = '70cm';
   189→      else if (r.frequency_mhz >= 1240 && r.frequency_mhz < 1300) band = '23cm';
   190→    }
   191→
   192→    adif += adifField('CALL', r.callsign);
   193→    adif += adifField('QSO_DATE', qsoDate);
   194→    adif += adifField('TIME_ON', timeOn);
   195→    adif += adifField('MODE', 'FM');
   196→    if (band) adif += adifField('BAND', band);
   197→    if (freq) adif += adifField('FREQ', freq);
   198→    adif += adifField('RST_SENT', rst);
   199→    adif += adifField('RST_RCVD', rst);
   200→    if (r.op_name) adif += adifField('NAME', r.op_name);
   201→    if (r.qth) adif += adifField('QTH', r.qth);
   202→    if (r.notes) adif += adifField('COMMENT', r.notes);
   203→    adif += '<EOR>\n';
   204→  }
   205→
   206→  const filename = `BOS-ARSA_QSOs_${from}_${to}.adi`;
   207→  res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'text/plain; charset=utf-8');
   208→  res.setHeader('Content-Disposition', `attachment; filename="${filename}"`);
   209→  res.send(adif);
   210→});
   211→
   212→// Placeholder fallback
   213→reportsRouter.get('/', (_req, res) => {
   214→  res.json({ message: 'Use /api/v1/reports/stats?from=YYYY-MM-DD&to=YYYY-MM-DD' });
   215→});
   216→

(oe_link_enabled ? 1 : 0) : 1, exercise_type || 'Krisenkommunikationsübung', simplex_bezirke ? 1 : 0, simplex_bl_codes || null);

    46→      r.bundesland_code, bl.name as bundesland_name
    47→    FROM exercise_repeaters er

    60→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id

   153→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports

   157→    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0

    37→
    38→function formatDateDE(d: string) {
    39→  const date = new Date(d + 'T00:00:00');
    40→  return date.toLocaleDateString('de-AT', { day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric' });
    41→}
    42→
    43→function toISO(d: Date) {
    44→  return d.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    45→}
    46→
    47→export default function AggregatedReportsPage() {
    48→  const now = new Date();
    49→  const year = now.getFullYear();
    50→
    51→  const [from, setFrom] = useState(() => toISO(new Date(year, 0, 1)));
    52→  const [to, setTo] = useState(() => toISO(new Date(year, 11, 31)));
    53→  const [data, setData] = useState<AggregatedResponse | null>(null);
    54→  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
    55→  const [showExercises, setShowExercises] = useState(false);
    56→  const [showParticipants, setShowParticipants] = useState(true);

    60→  const barChartRef = useRef<any>(null);
    61→  const pieChartRef = useRef<any>(null);
    62→  const barCardRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    63→  const pieCardRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    64→  const mapCardRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    65→  const pageRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    66→
    67→  const fetchStats = (f: string, t: string, types?: Set<string>) => {
    68→    setLoading(true);
    69→    const typesParam = types && types.size > 0 ? `&types=${Array.from(types).map(encodeURIComponent).join(',')}` : '';
    70→    apiFetch(`/api/v1/reports/stats?from=${f}&to=${t}${typesParam}`)
    71→      .then((res) => setData(res))
    72→      .catch(() => setData(null))
    73→      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
    74→  };
    75→
    76→  useEffect(() => {
    77→    fetchStats(from, to, selectedTypes);

    79→

    81→    setFrom(f);

    86→  const applyQuarter = (q: number) => {

    89→    applyRange(toISO(start), toISO(end));
    90→  };
    91→
    92→  const applyYear = () => {
    93→    applyRange(toISO(new Date(year, 0, 1)), toISO(new Date(year, 11, 31)));

   145→  const downloadChart = async (cardRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>, filename: string) => {
   146→    if (!cardRef.current) return;
   147→    setExporting(true);
   148→    const header = createExportHeader();

   154→    parent.insertBefore(wrapper, cardRef.current);

   164→    } catch {} finally {
   165→      if (next) parent.insertBefore(cardRef.current, next); else parent.appendChild(cardRef.current);

   200→  const stats = data?.stats ?? null;

   667→                    if (!res.ok) throw new Error();

   670→                    const link = document.createElement('a');
   671→                    link.href = url;
   672→                    link.download = `BOS-ARSA_QSOs_${from}_${to}.adi`;
   673→                    link.click();
   674→                    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

   677→                className="bg-red-700 hover:bg-red-800 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-medium"
   678→              >

    68→    const parent = cardRef.current.parentNode!;

   223→            const chartLevel = stats.bezirkStats.length > 0 ? 'Bezirk' : 'Bundesland';
   224→
   225→            return chartData.length > 0 ? (
   226→            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
   227→              {/* Bar chart: Stationen + Rapporte */}
   228→              <div ref={barCardRef} className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-3 sm:p-4">
   229→                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2 sm:mb-3">
   230→                  <h2 className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-[#1e3a5f]">
   231→                    BOS-ARSA Übung am {new Date(exercise.date + 'T00:00:00').toLocaleDateString('de-AT')}
   232→                  </h2>
   233→                  <button
   234→                    onClick={() => downloadChart(barCardRef, `BOS-ARSA_Stationen_${exercise.date}.png`)}
   235→                    className="text-xs text-gray-400 hover:text-blue-600 flex-shrink-0 ml-2"
   236→                    title="Grafik als PNG herunterladen"
   237→                    data-no-export="true"
   238→                  >
   239→                    Download
   240→                  </button>
   241→                </div>
   242→                <div className="h-48 sm:h-[350px]">
   243→                  <Bar
   244→                    ref={barChartRef}
   245→                    data={{
   246→                      labels: chartData.map(d => d.label),
   247→                      datasets: [
   248→                        {
   249→                          label: 'Stationen',
   250→                          data: chartData.map(d => d.participants),
   251→                          backgroundColor: '#1e3a5f',
   252→                        },
   253→                        {
   254→                          label: 'Rapporte',
   255→                          data: chartData.map(d => d.reports),
   256→                          backgroundColor: '#c8102e',
   257→                        },
   258→                      ],
   259→                    }}
   260→                    options={{
   261→                      responsive: true,
   262→                      maintainAspectRatio: false,
   263→                      plugins: { legend: { position: 'bottom' } },
   264→                      scales: {
   265→                        x: { ticks: { maxRotation: 90, minRotation: 45, font: { size: 9 } } },
   266→                        y: { beginAtZero: true },
   267→                      },
   268→                    }}
   269→                  />
   270→                </div>
   271→              </div>
   272→
   273→              {/* Pie chart: Rapporte distribution */}
   274→              <div ref={pieCardRef} className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-3 sm:p-4">
   275→                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2 sm:mb-3">
   276→                  <h2 className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-[#1e3a5f]">
   277→                    Rapporte Verteilung nach Bundesland / Land
   278→                  </h2>
   279→                  <button
   280→                    onClick={() => downloadChart(pieCardRef, `BOS-ARSA_Verteilung_${exercise.date}.png`)}
   281→                    className="text-xs text-gray-400 hover:text-blue-600 flex-shrink-0 ml-2"
   282→                    title="Grafik als PNG herunterladen"
   283→                    data-no-export="true"
   284→                  >
   285→                    Download
   286→                  </button>
   287→                </div>
   288→                <div className="h-48 sm:h-[350px] flex items-center justify-center">
   289→                  <Pie
   290→                    ref={pieChartRef}
   291→                    data={{
   292→                      labels: chartData.filter(d => d.reports > 0).map(d => d.label),
   293→                      datasets: [{
   294→                        data: chartData.filter(d => d.reports > 0).map(d => d.reports),
   295→                        backgroundColor: [
   296→                          '#1e3a5f', '#c8102e', '#d97706', '#0d6efd', '#198754',
   297→                          '#6f42c1', '#fd7e14', '#20c997', '#0dcaf0', '#6c757d',
   298→                          '#2c5282', '#e8a317', '#c70039', '#4361ee', '#2d6a4f',
   299→                          '#9b59b6', '#e67e22', '#1abc9c', '#3498db', '#95a5a6',
   300→                        ],
   301→                      }],
   302→                    }}
   303→                    options={{
   304→                      responsive: true,
   305→                      maintainAspectRatio: false,
   306→                      plugins: {
   307→                        legend: { position: 'right', labels: { font: { size: 10 } } },
   308→                      },
   309→                    }}
   310→                  />
   311→                </div>
   312→              </div>
   313→            </div>
   314→          ) : null;
   315→          })()}
   316→
   317→          {/* Bundesland table (when no bezirk data) */}
   318→          {stats.bezirkStats.length === 0 && stats.blStats.length > 0 && (
   319→            <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow overflow-hidden">
   320→              <div className="bg-[#1e3a5f] text-white px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold">
   321→                Teilnehmer nach Bundesland / Land
   322→              </div>
   323→              <table className="w-full text-sm">
   324→                <thead>
   325→                  <tr className="bg-gray-50 border-b text-left text-xs text-gray-500 uppercase">
   326→                    <th className="px-4 py-2">Bundesland</th>
   327→                    <th className="px-4 py-2 text-right w-28">Teilnehmer</th>
   328→                    <th className="px-4 py-2 text-right w-28">Rapporte</th>
   329→                  </tr>
   330→                </thead>
   331→                <tbody>
   332→                  {stats.blStats.map(bl => (
   333→                    <tr key={bl.bundesland_code} className="border-t hover:bg-blue-50">
   334→                      <td className="px-4 py-1.5 font-medium">{bl.bundesland}</td>
   335→                      <td className="px-4 py-1.5 text-right">{bl.participants}</td>
   336→                      <td className="px-4 py-1.5 text-right">{bl.reports}</td>
   337→                    </tr>
   338→                  ))}
   339→                  <tr className="bg-[#1e3a5f] text-white font-bold">
   340→                    <td className="px-4 py-2">Gesamt</td>
   341→                    <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right">{stats.totalParticipants}</td>
   342→                    <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right">{stats.totalReports}</td>
   343→                  </tr>
   344→                </tbody>
   345→              </table>
   346→            </div>
   347→          )}
   348→
   349→          {/* Bezirk/Bundesland table */}
   350→          {stats.bezirkStats.length > 0 && (
   351→            <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow overflow-hidden">
   352→              <div className="bg-[#1e3a5f] text-white px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold">
   353→                Nebenstationen nach Bezirk
   354→              </div>
   355→              <div className="overflow-x-auto">
   356→              <table className="w-full text-xs sm:text-sm min-w-[360px]">
   357→                <thead>
   358→                  <tr className="bg-gray-50 border-b text-left text-xs text-gray-500 uppercase">
   359→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2">Bundesland / Bezirk</th>
   360→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 text-right w-16 sm:w-28">Teiln.</th>
   361→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 text-right w-16 sm:w-28">Rapp.</th>
   362→                  </tr>
   363→                </thead>
   364→                <tbody>
   365→                  {Object.entries(byBl).map(([blCode, bl]) => (
   366→                    <>
   367→                      {/* Bundesland header */}
   368→                      <tr key={`bl-${blCode}`} className="bg-gray-50 font-medium border-t">
   369→                        <td className="px-4 py-1.5">{bl.name}</td>
   370→                        <td className="px-4 py-1.5 text-right font-bold">{bl.totalParticipants}</td>
   371→                        <td className="px-4 py-1.5 text-right font-bold">{bl.totalReports}</td>
   372→                      </tr>
   373→                      {/* Bezirk rows */}
   374→                      {bl.bezirke.map(bz => (
   375→                        <tr key={bz.bezirk_code} className="border-t border-gray-100 hover:bg-blue-50">
   376→                          <td className="px-4 py-1 pl-8">
   377→                            <span className={`inline-block text-xs font-mono px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-white mr-2 ${bz.is_capital ? 'bg-[#dc3545]' : 'bg-[#6c757d]'}`}>
   378→                              {bz.bezirk_code}
   379→                            </span>
   380→                            <span className="text-gray-600">{bz.bezirk_name}</span>
   381→                          </td>
   382→                          <td className="px-4 py-1 text-right">{bz.participants}</td>
   383→                          <td className="px-4 py-1 text-right">{bz.reports}</td>
   384→                        </tr>
   385→                      ))}
   386→                    </>
   387→                  ))}
   388→                  {/* Gesamt */}
   389→                  <tr className="bg-[#1e3a5f] text-white font-bold">
   390→                    <td className="px-4 py-2">Gesamt</td>
   391→                    <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right">{stats.totalParticipants}</td>
   392→                    <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right">{stats.totalReports}</td>
   393→                  </tr>
   394→                </tbody>
   395→              </table>
   396→              </div>
   397→            </div>
   398→          )}
   399→
   400→          {/* Participants list */}
   401→          <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow overflow-hidden">
   402→            <div
   403→              onClick={() => setShowParticipants(v => !v)}
   404→              className="bg-[#1e3a5f] text-white px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold cursor-pointer hover:bg-[#2a4a7f] flex items-center justify-between"
   405→            >
   406→              <span>Teilnehmer-Liste ({stats.participants.length})</span>
   407→              <span className="text-xs">{showParticipants ? '▼' : '▶'}</span>
   408→            </div>
   409→            {showParticipants && (
   410→              <div className="overflow-x-auto">
   411→              <table className="w-full text-xs sm:text-sm min-w-[360px]">
   412→                <thead>
   413→                  <tr className="bg-gray-50 border-b text-left text-xs text-gray-500 uppercase">
   414→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 w-8">#</th>
   415→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2">Rufzeichen</th>
   416→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 hidden sm:table-cell">Name</th>
   417→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2">Bezirk</th>
   418→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 hidden md:table-cell">Bundesland / Land</th>
   419→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 text-right w-16 sm:w-24">Rapp.</th>
   420→                  </tr>
   421→                </thead>
   422→                <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
   423→                  {stats.participants.map((p, idx) => (
   424→                    <tr key={p.callsign} className="hover:bg-blue-50">
   425→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 text-xs text-gray-400">{idx + 1}</td>
   426→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 font-mono font-medium">{p.callsign}</td>
   427→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 text-gray-600 hidden sm:table-cell">{p.name || ''}</td>
   428→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1">
   429→                        {p.bezirk_code && (
   430→                          <span className="text-xs bg-gray-200 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono">{p.bezirk_code}</span>
   431→                        )}
   432→                      </td>
   433→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 text-gray-600 text-xs hidden md:table-cell">{p.bundesland_name || ''}</td>
   434→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 text-right font-mono">{p.report_count}</td>
   435→                    </tr>
   436→                  ))}
   437→                </tbody>
   438→              </table>
   439→              </div>
   440→            )}
   441→          </div>
   442→
   443→          {/* Export buttons */}
   444→          <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-4" data-no-export="true">
   445→            <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-[#1e3a5f] mb-3">Export</h2>
   446→            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
   447→              <button
   448→                onClick={downloadFullPage}
   449→                disabled={exporting}
   450→                className="bg-[#1e3a5f] hover:bg-[#2a4a7f] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-medium disabled:opacity-50"
   451→              >
   452→                {exporting ? 'Exportieren...' : 'PNG — Download Auswertung'}
   453→              </button>
   454→              {exercise.oe_link_enabled !== 0 && (
   455→                <a
   456→                  href={`/api/v1/export/exercises/${id}/bund`}
   457→                  className="bg-red-100 hover:bg-red-200 text-red-800 px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-medium"
   458→                  target="_blank"
   459→                >
   460→                  TXT — OE-Link
   461→                </a>
   462→              )}
   463→              <a
   464→                href={`/api/v1/export/exercises/${id}/land`}
   465→                className="bg-red-100 hover:bg-red-200 text-red-800 px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-medium"
   466→                target="_blank"
   467→              >
   468→                TXT — Frequenzen
   469→              </a>
   470→              <a
   471→                href={`/api/v1/export/exercises/${id}/combined`}
   472→                className="bg-red-100 hover:bg-red-200 text-red-800 px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-medium"
   473→                target="_blank"
   474→              >
   475→                TXT — Kombiniert
   476→              </a>
   477→            </div>
   478→          </div>
   479→        </>
   480→      )}
   481→    </div>
   482→  );
   483→}
   484→

   147→
   148→function getColor(value: number, max: number): string {
   149→  if (max === 0) return '#f3f4f6';
   150→  if (value === 0) return '#f3f4f6';
   151→  const t = value / max;
   152→  const r = Math.round(194 + (30 - 194) * t);
   153→  const g = Math.round(213 + (58 - 213) * t);
   154→  const b = Math.round(236 + (95 - 236) * t);
   155→  return `rgb(${r},${g},${b})`;
   156→}
   157→
   158→export default function AustriaMap({ data }: Props) {
   159→  const [tooltip, setTooltip] = useState<{
   160→    x: number;
   161→    y: number;
   162→    code: string;
   163→    name: string;
   164→    participants: number;
   165→    reports: number;
   166→  } | null>(null);
   167→
   168→  const dataMap = new Map(data.map((d) => [d.code, d]));
   169→  const maxParticipants = Math.max(...data.map((d) => d.participants), 1);
   170→
   171→  const showTooltip = (e: React.MouseEvent, code: string, labelX: number, labelY: number, participants: number, reports: number) => {
   172→    const svg = e.currentTarget.closest('svg');
   173→    if (!svg) return;
   174→    const rect = svg.getBoundingClientRect();
   175→    const pt = svg.createSVGPoint();
   176→    pt.x = labelX;
   177→    pt.y = labelY;
   178→    const ctm = svg.getScreenCTM();
   179→    if (!ctm) return;
   180→    const screenPt = pt.matrixTransform(ctm);
   181→    setTooltip({
   182→      x: screenPt.x - rect.left,
   183→      y: screenPt.y - rect.top - 10,
   184→      code: BL_SHORT[code] || code,
   185→      name: BL_NAMES[code] || code,
   186→      participants,
   187→      reports,
   188→    });
   189→  };
   190→
   191→  return (
   192→    <div className="relative">
   193→      <svg
   194→        viewBox="-300 -120 1100 560"
   195→        className="w-full h-auto"
   196→        role="img"
   197→        aria-label="Karte von Österreich und Nachbarländer mit Teilnehmern"
   198→      >
   199→        {/* Clip to visible area */}

But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.
</system-reminder>

```

     1→export async function getOrCreateOperator(callsign: string, existing: any): Promise<any> {
     2→  if (existing) return existing;
     3→  if (!callsign || callsign.length < 3) return null;
     4→  try {
     5→    return await apiFetch('/api/v1/operators', {
     6→      method: 'POST',
     7→      body: JSON.stringify({ callsign: callsign.toUpperCase() }),
     8→    });
     9→  } catch (err: any) {
    10→    if (err.message?.includes('existiert')) {
    11→      const results = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators?q=${encodeURIComponent(callsign)}`);
    12→      return results.find((o: any) => o.callsign === callsign.toUpperCase()) || results[0] || null;
    13→    }
    14→    return null;
    15→  }
    16→}
    17→
    18→export async function apiFetch(url: string, options?: RequestInit) {
    19→  const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
    20→  const res = await fetch(url, {
    21→    ...options,
    22→    headers: {
    23→      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    24→      ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
    25→      ...options?.headers,
    26→    },
    27→  });
    28→  if (res.status === 401) {
    29→    localStorage.removeItem('token');
    30→    localStorage.removeItem('admin');
    31→    window.location.href = '/login';
    32→    throw new Error('Unauthorized');
    33→  }
    34→  if (!res.ok) {
    35→    const err = await res.json().catch(() => ({ error: 'Fehler' }));
    36→    const error: any = new Error(err.error || `HTTP ${res.status}`);
    37→    error.data = err;
    38→    throw error;
    39→  }
    40→  return res.json();
    41→}
    42→

'08': 'Kärnten',\n  '09': 'Vorarlberg',\n};\n\n// ── Helper: color interpolation (same as AustriaMap.tsx) ──\nfunction getHeatColor(value: number, max: number): string {\n  if (max === 0 || value === 0) return '#e5e7eb';\n  const t = value / max;\n  const r = Math.round(194 + (30 - 194) * t);\n  const g = Math.round(213 + (58 - 213) * t);\n  const b = Math.round(236 + (95 - 236) * t);\n  return `rgb(${r},${g},${b})`;\n}\n\nfunction hexToRgb(hex: string): [number, number, number] {\n  const h = hex.replace('#', '');\n  return [parseInt(h.slice(0, 2), 16), parseInt(h.slice(2, 4), 16), parseInt(h.slice(4, 6), 16)];\n}\n\nfunction formatDateDE(d: string): string {\n  const date = new Date(d + 'T00:00:00');\n  return date.toLocaleDateString('de-AT', { day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric' });\n}\n\n// ── Page break helper ──────────────────────────────────────\nfunction ensureSpace(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, needed: number) {\n  const bottom = doc.page.height - doc.page.margins.bottom;\n  if (doc.y + needed > bottom) {\n    doc.addPage();\n  }\n}\n\n// ── Render functions ─────────────────────────────────────\n\nfunction renderHeader(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, title: string, subtitle: string) {\n  const pageW = doc.page.width;\n  const m = doc.page.margins;\n  doc.save();\n  doc.rect(m.left, m.top, pageW - m.left - m.right, 50).fill(NAVY);\n  doc.fontSize(18).fillColor(WHITE).text('BOS-ARSA Log', m.left + 15, m.top + 10, { continued: false });\n  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor('#93c5fd').text('Im Sinne der Sicherheit', m.left + 15, m.top + 32);\n  doc.restore();\n  doc.y = m.top + 60;\n  doc.fontSize(14).fillColor(NAVY).text(title, m.left, doc.y);\n  doc.fontSize(9).fillColor(GRAY_500).text(subtitle, m.left, doc.y);\n  doc.moveDown(0.8);\n}\n\nfunction renderSummaryCards(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, stats: PdfStats, exerciseCount?: number) {\n  ensureSpace(doc, 50);\n  const m = doc.page.margins;\n  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;\n  const cols = exerciseCount !== undefined ? 4 : 2;\n  const cardW = usable / cols - 8;\n  const startX = m.left;\n  const startY = doc.y;\n\n  const cards: { value: string | number; label: string }[] = [\n    { value: stats.totalParticipants, label: 'Teilnehmer' },\n    { value: stats.totalReports, label: 'Rapporte' },\n  ];\n  if (exerciseCount !== undefined) {\n    cards.push({ value: exerciseCount, label: 'Übungen' });\n    const avg = exerciseCount > 0 ? Math.round(stats.totalParticipants / exerciseCount * 10) / 10 : 0;\n    cards.push({ value: avg, label: 'Ø Teiln./Übung' });\n  }\n\n  cards.forEach((c, i) => {\n    const x = startX + i * (cardW + 8);\n    doc.save();\n    doc.roundedRect(x, startY, cardW, 40, 4).fill(GRAY_50);\n    doc.fontSize(18).fillColor(NAVY).text(String(c.value), x, startY + 5, { width: cardW, align: 'center' });\n    doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(GRAY_500).text(c.label, x, startY + 27, { width: cardW, align: 'center' });\n    doc.restore();\n  });\n  doc.y = startY + 48;\n}\n\nfunction renderPerRepeater(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, perRepeater: PerRepeater[]) {\n  if (perRepeater.length === 0) return;\n  ensureSpace(doc, 35);\n  const m = doc.page.margins;\n  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;\n  const cols = Math.min(perRepeater.length, 6);\n  const cardW = usable / cols - 6;\n  const startX = m.left;\n  const startY = doc.y;\n\n  perRepeater.slice(0, 6).forEach((r, i) => {\n    const x = startX + i * (cardW + 6);\n    doc.save();\n    doc.roundedRect(x, startY, cardW, 28, 3).fill(GRAY_50);\n    doc.fontSize(12).fillColor(GRAY_500).text(String(r.count), x, startY + 2, { width: cardW, align: 'center' });\n    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500).text(r.short_name, x, startY + 17, { width: cardW, align: 'center' });\n    doc.restore();\n  });\n  doc.y = startY + 36;\n}\n\nfunction renderExerciseTable(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, exercises: ExerciseSummary[]) {\n  if (exercises.length === 0) return;\n  ensureSpace(doc, 60);\n  const m = doc.page.margins;\n  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;\n\n  // Section header\n  doc.save();\n  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 18).fill(NAVY);\n  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor(WHITE).text(`Übungen (${exercises.length})`, m.left + 8, doc.y + 4);\n  doc.restore();\n  doc.y += 20;\n\n  // Column headers\n  const colDate = m.left + 5;\n  const colName = m.left + 80;\n  const colPart = m.left + usable - 90;\n  const colRap = m.left + usable - 40;\n\n  doc.save();\n  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 14).fill(GRAY_100);\n  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);\n  doc.text('DATUM', colDate, doc.y + 4);\n  doc.text('NAME', colName, doc.y + 4);\n  doc.text('TEILN.', colPart, doc.y + 4, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n  doc.text('RAPP.', colRap, doc.y + 4, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n  doc.restore();\n  doc.y += 16;\n\n  exercises.forEach((ex, i) => {\n    ensureSpace(doc, 14);\n    if (i % 2 === 0) {\n      doc.save();\n      doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 12).fill(GRAY_50);\n      doc.restore();\n    }\n    doc.fontSize(7).fillColor('#111827');\n    doc.text(formatDateDE(ex.date), colDate, doc.y + 2);\n    doc.text(ex.name || '', colName, doc.y + 2, { width: colPart - colName - 10 });\n    doc.text(String(ex.participant_count), colPart, doc.y + 2, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n    doc.text(String(ex.report_count), colRap, doc.y + 2, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n    doc.y += 13;\n  });\n  doc.moveDown(0.5);\n}\n\nfunction renderAustriaMap(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, blStats: BlStat[]) {\n  if (blStats.length === 0) return;\n  ensureSpace(doc, 200);\n\n  const m = doc.page.margins;\n  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;\n  const mapW = Math.min(usable, 400);\n  const mapH = mapW * 0.5;\n  const offsetX = m.left + (usable - mapW) / 2;\n  const offsetY = doc.y;\n\n  // Section header\n  doc.save();\n  doc.fontSize(9).fillColor(NAVY).text('Teilnehmer nach Bundesland', m.left, doc.y);\n  doc.restore();\n  doc.y += 14;\n  const mapStartY = doc.y;\n\n  const dataMap = new Map(blStats.map(bl => [bl.bundesland_code, bl]));\n  const maxP = Math.max(...blStats.map(bl => bl.participants), 1);\n\n  // SVG viewBox: roughly 0,0 to 620,300 for Austrian paths\n  // Scale to fit mapW x mapH\n  const svgW = 620;\n  const svgH = 300;\n  const scale = Math.min(mapW / svgW, mapH / svgH);\n\n  // Parse and draw SVG paths\n  for (const [code, pathData] of Object.entries(BL_PATHS)) {\n    const bl = dataMap.get(code);\n    const participants = bl?.participants ?? 0;\n    const fill = getHeatColor(participants, maxP);\n\n    // Parse the SVG path and draw with PDFKit\n    doc.save();\n    doc.translate(offsetX, mapStartY);\n    doc.scale(scale);\n\n    doc.path(pathData.d).fill(fill).stroke(WHITE);\n    doc.restore();\n\n    // Draw label\n    const lx = offsetX + pathData.labelX * scale;\n    const ly = mapStartY + pathData.labelY * scale;\n    const label = BL_SHORT[code] || code;\n    doc.save();\n    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(NAVY).text(label, lx - 15, ly - 6, { width: 30, align: 'center' });\n    doc.fontSize(5).fillColor(NAVY).text(String(participants), lx - 15, ly + 1, { width: 30, align: 'center' });\n    doc.restore();\n  }\n\n  doc.y = mapStartY + mapH + 10;\n}\n\nfunction renderBarChart(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, chartData: { label: string; participants: number; reports: number }[]) {\n  if (chartData.length === 0) return;\n  ensureSpace(doc, 180);\n\n  const m = doc.page.margins;\n  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;\n  const chartW = Math.min(usable, 500);\n  const chartH = 140;\n  const startX = m.left;\n  const startY = doc.y + 15;\n\n  doc.fontSize(9).fillColor(NAVY).text('Stationen & Rapporte', m.left, doc.y);\n  doc.y = startY;\n\n  const maxVal = Math.max(...chartData.map(d => Math.max(d.participants, d.reports)), 1);\n  const barAreaW = chartW - 30; // leave room for y-axis\n  const groupW = barAreaW / chartData.length;\n  const barW = Math.min(groupW * 0.35, 20);\n\n  // Y-axis gridlines\n  for (let i = 0; i <= 4; i++) {\n    const y = startY + chartH - (chartH * i / 4);\n    const val = Math.round(maxVal * i / 4);\n    doc.save();\n    doc.strokeColor(GRAY_200).lineWidth(0.5).moveTo(startX + 25, y).lineTo(startX + chartW, y).stroke();\n    doc.fontSize(5).fillColor(GRAY_500).text(String(val), startX, y - 3, { width: 22, align: 'right' });\n    doc.restore();\n  }\n\n  // Bars\n  chartData.forEach((d, i) => {\n    const groupX = startX + 30 + i * groupW;\n\n    // Participants bar\n    const pH = (d.participants / maxVal) * chartH;\n    doc.save();\n    doc.rect(groupX, startY + chartH - pH, barW, pH).fill(NAVY);\n    doc.restore();\n\n    // Reports bar\n    const rH = (d.reports / maxVal) * chartH;\n    doc.save();\n    doc.rect(groupX + barW + 2, startY + chartH - rH, barW, rH).fill(RED);\n    doc.restore();\n\n    // Label\n    doc.save();\n    doc.fontSize(4).fillColor(GRAY_500);\n    doc.text(d.label, groupX - 2, startY + chartH + 3, { width: groupW, align: 'left' });\n    doc.restore();\n  });\n\n  // Legend\n  const legendY = startY + chartH + 14;\n  doc.save();\n  doc.rect(startX + 30, legendY, 8, 6).fill(NAVY);\n  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor('#111827').text('Stationen', startX + 42, legendY);\n  doc.rect(startX + 90, legendY, 8, 6).fill(RED);\n  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor('#111827').text('Rapporte', startX + 102, legendY);\n  doc.restore();\n\n  doc.y = legendY + 14;\n}\n\nfunction renderPieChart(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, chartData: { label: string; value: number }[]) {\n  const filtered = chartData.filter(d => d.value > 0);\n  if (filtered.length === 0) return;\n  ensureSpace(doc, 180);\n\n  const m = doc.page.margins;\n  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;\n\n  doc.fontSize(9).fillColor(NAVY).text('Rapporte Verteilung', m.left, doc.y);\n  doc.moveDown(0.3);\n\n  const cx = m.left + 100;\n  const cy = doc.y + 70;\n  const r = 60;\n  const total = filtered.reduce((s, d) => s + d.value, 0);\n  let startAngle = -Math.PI / 2;\n\n  filtered.forEach((d, i) => {\n    const sliceAngle = (d.value / total) * 2 * Math.PI;\n    const endAngle = startAngle + sliceAngle;\n    const color = PIE_COLORS[i % PIE_COLORS.length];\n\n    // Draw pie slice using path\n    const x1 = cx + r * Math.cos(startAngle);\n    const y1 = cy + r * Math.sin(startAngle);\n    const x2 = cx + r * Math.cos(endAngle);\n    const y2 = cy + r * Math.sin(endAngle);\n    const largeArc = sliceAngle > Math.PI ? 1 : 0;\n\n    doc.save();\n    doc.path(`M ${cx} ${cy} L ${x1} ${y1} A ${r} ${r} 0 ${largeArc} 1 ${x2} ${y2} Z`).fill(color);\n    doc.restore();\n\n    startAngle = endAngle;\n  });\n\n  // Legend on the right\n  const legendX = cx + r + 30;\n  filtered.forEach((d, i) => {\n    const ly = cy - r + i * 12;\n    if (ly > doc.page.height - m.bottom) return;\n    const color = PIE_COLORS[i % PIE_COLORS.length];\n    doc.save();\n    doc.rect(legendX, ly, 8, 8).fill(color);\n    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor('#111827').text(`${d.label} (${d.value})`, legendX + 12, ly + 1, { width: usable - legendX + m.left - 12 });\n    doc.restore();\n  });\n\n  doc.y = cy + r + 15;\n}\n\nfunction renderBezirkTable(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, bezirkStats: BezirkStat[], stats: PdfStats) {\n  if (bezirkStats.length === 0) return;\n  ensureSpace(doc, 60);\n\n  const m = doc.page.margins;\n  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;\n\n  // Section header\n  doc.save();\n  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 18).fill(NAVY);\n  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor(WHITE).text('Nebenstationen nach Bezirk', m.left + 8, doc.y + 4);\n  doc.restore();\n  doc.y += 20;\n\n  // Column headers\n  const colName = m.left + 5;\n  const colPart = m.left + usable - 90;\n  const colRap = m.left + usable - 40;\n\n  doc.save();\n  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 14).fill(GRAY_100);\n  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);\n  doc.text('BUNDESLAND / BEZIRK', colName, doc.y + 4);\n  doc.text('TEILN.', colPart, doc.y + 4, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n  doc.text('RAPP.', colRap, doc.y + 4, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n  doc.restore();\n  doc.y += 16;\n\n  // Group by Bundesland\n  const byBl: Record<string, { name: string; bezirke: BezirkStat[]; totalP: number; totalR: number }> = {};\n  for (const bz of bezirkStats) {\n    if (!byBl[bz.bundesland_code]) {\n      byBl[bz.bundesland_code] = { name: bz.bundesland, bezirke: [], totalP: 0, totalR: 0 };\n    }\n    byBl[bz.bundesland_code].bezirke.push(bz);\n    byBl[bz.bundesland_code].totalP += bz.participants;\n    byBl[bz.bundesland_code].totalR += bz.reports;\n  }\n\n  for (const [, bl] of Object.entries(byBl)) {\n    ensureSpace(doc, 14);\n    // BL header row\n    doc.save();\n    doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 13).fill(GRAY_100);\n    doc.fontSize(7).fillColor('#111827').text(bl.name, colName, doc.y + 3, { bold: true } as any);\n    doc.text(String(bl.totalP), colPart, doc.y + 3, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n    doc.text(String(bl.totalR), colRap, doc.y + 3, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n    doc.restore();\n    doc.y += 14;\n\n    // Bezirk rows\n    bl.bezirke.forEach(bz => {\n      ensureSpace(doc, 12);\n      doc.fontSize(6).fillColor('#374151');\n      doc.text(`    ${bz.bezirk_code}  ${bz.bezirk_name}`, colName, doc.y + 1);\n      doc.text(String(bz.participants), colPart, doc.y + 1, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n      doc.text(String(bz.reports), colRap, doc.y + 1, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n      doc.y += 11;\n    });\n  }\n\n  // Gesamt row\n  ensureSpace(doc, 18);\n  doc.save();\n  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 16).fill(NAVY);\n  doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(WHITE).text('Gesamt', colName, doc.y + 4);\n  doc.text(String(stats.totalParticipants), colPart, doc.y + 4, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n  doc.text(String(stats.totalReports), colRap, doc.y + 4, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n  doc.restore();\n  doc.y += 20;\n}\n\nfunction renderBlTable(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, blStats: BlStat[], stats: PdfStats) {\n  if (blStats.length === 0) return;\n  ensureSpace(doc, 60);\n\n  const m = doc.page.margins;\n  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;\n\n  // Section header\n  doc.save();\n  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 18).fill(NAVY);\n  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor(WHITE).text('Teilnehmer nach Bundesland / Land', m.left + 8, doc.y + 4);\n  doc.restore();\n  doc.y += 20;\n\n  const colName = m.left + 5;\n  const colPart = m.left + usable - 90;\n  const colRap = m.left + usable - 40;\n\n  doc.save();\n  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 14).fill(GRAY_100);\n  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);\n  doc.text('BUNDESLAND', colName, doc.y + 4);\n  doc.text('TEILNEHMER', colPart, doc.y + 4, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n  doc.text('RAPPORTE', colRap, doc.y + 4, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n  doc.restore();\n  doc.y += 16;\n\n  blStats.forEach((bl, i) => {\n    ensureSpace(doc, 12);\n    if (i % 2 === 0) {\n      doc.save();\n      doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 12).fill(GRAY_50);\n      doc.restore();\n    }\n    doc.fontSize(7).fillColor('#111827');\n    doc.text(bl.bundesland, colName, doc.y + 2);\n    doc.text(String(bl.participants), colPart, doc.y + 2, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n    doc.text(String(bl.reports), colRap, doc.y + 2, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n    doc.y += 13;\n  });\n\n  // Gesamt\n  ensureSpace(doc, 18);\n  doc.save();\n  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 16).fill(NAVY);\n  doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(WHITE).text('Gesamt', colName, doc.y + 4);\n  doc.text(String(stats.totalParticipants), colPart, doc.y + 4, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n  doc.text(String(stats.totalReports), colRap, doc.y + 4, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n  doc.restore();\n  doc.y += 20;\n}\n\nfunction renderParticipants(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, participants: Participant[]) {\n  if (participants.length === 0) return;\n  ensureSpace(doc, 60);\n\n  const m = doc.page.margins;\n  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;\n\n  // Section header\n  doc.save();\n  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 18).fill(NAVY);\n  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor(WHITE).text(`Teilnehmer-Liste (${participants.length})`, m.left + 8, doc.y + 4);\n  doc.restore();\n  doc.y += 20;\n\n  // Column headers\n  const colNum = m.left + 3;\n  const colCall = m.left + 25;\n  const colName = m.left + 90;\n  const colBz = m.left + usable - 135;\n  const colBl = m.left + usable - 90;\n  const colRap = m.left + usable - 35;\n\n  doc.save();\n  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 14).fill(GRAY_100);\n  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);\n  doc.text('#', colNum, doc.y + 4);\n  doc.text('RUFZEICHEN', colCall, doc.y + 4);\n  doc.text('NAME', colName, doc.y + 4);\n  doc.text('BEZ.', colBz, doc.y + 4);\n  doc.text('BUNDESLAND', colBl, doc.y + 4);\n  doc.text('RAPP.', colRap, doc.y + 4, { width: 35, align: 'right' });\n  doc.restore();\n  doc.y += 16;\n\n  participants.forEach((p, i) => {\n    ensureSpace(doc, 11);\n    if (i % 2 === 0) {\n      doc.save();\n      doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 10).fill(GRAY_50);\n      doc.restore();\n    }\n    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500).text(String(i + 1), colNum, doc.y + 2);\n    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor('#111827').text(p.callsign, colCall, doc.y + 2);\n    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor('#374151').text(p.name || '', colName, doc.y + 2, { width: colBz - colName - 5 });\n    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor('#374151').text(p.bezirk_code || '', colBz, doc.y + 2);\n    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor('#374151').text(p.bundesland_name || '', colBl, doc.y + 2, { width: colRap - colBl - 5 });\n    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor('#111827').text(String(p.report_count), colRap, doc.y + 2, { width: 35, align: 'right' });\n    doc.y += 11;\n  });\n  doc.moveDown(0.5);\n}\n\nfunction renderFooter(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument) {\n  const m = doc.page.margins;\n  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;\n  ensureSpace(doc, 25);\n  doc.save();\n  doc.moveTo(m.left, doc.y).lineTo(m.left + usable, doc.y).strokeColor(GRAY_200).lineWidth(0.5).stroke();\n  doc.y += 8;\n  doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(GRAY_500).text('oeradio.at  ·  bosarsa.oeradio.at', m.left, doc.y, { width: usable, align: 'center' });\n  doc.restore();\n}\n\n// ── Public API ────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nexport function generateAggregatedPdf(\n  exercises: ExerciseSummary[],\n  stats: PdfStats,\n  from: string,\n  to: string,\n): PDFKit.PDFDocument {\n  const doc = new PDFDocument({\n    layout: 'landscape',\n    size: 'A4',\n    margins: { top: 30, bottom: 30, left: 40, right: 40 },\n    autoFirstPage: true,\n    bufferPages: true,\n  });\n\n  renderHeader(doc, 'Auswertung', `${formatDateDE(from)} – ${formatDateDE(to)}  ·  ${exercises.length} Übung${exercises.length !== 1 ? 'en' : ''}`);\n  renderSummaryCards(doc, stats, exercises.length);\n  renderPerRepeater(doc, stats.perRepeater);\n  renderExerciseTable(doc, exercises);\n\n  // Visual section: map + charts\n  if (stats.blStats.length > 0) {\n    doc.addPage();\n    renderAustriaMap(doc, stats.blStats);\n  }\n\n  const chartData = stats.bezirkStats.length > 0\n    ? stats.bezirkStats.map(bz => ({ label: `${bz.bundesland} ${bz.bezirk_code}`, participants: bz.participants, reports: bz.reports }))\n    : stats.blStats.map(bl => ({ label: bl.bundesland, participants: bl.participants, reports: bl.reports }));\n\n  if (chartData.length > 0) {\n    renderBarChart(doc, chartData);\n    renderPieChart(doc, chartData.map(d => ({ label: d.label, value: d.reports })));\n  }\n\n  // Tables\n  if (stats.bezirkStats.length > 0) {\n    doc.addPage();\n    renderBezirkTable(doc, stats.bezirkStats, stats);\n  } else if (stats.blStats.length > 0) {\n    doc.addPage();\n    renderBlTable(doc, stats.blStats, stats);\n  }\n\n  // Participants\n  doc.addPage();\n  renderParticipants(doc, stats.participants);\n\n  renderFooter(doc);\n  doc.end();\n  return doc;\n}\n\nexport function generateExercisePdf(\n  exercise: { name: string; date: string; exercise_type?: string | null },\n  stats: PdfStats,\n): PDFKit.PDFDocument {\n  const doc = new PDFDocument({\n    layout: 'landscape',\n    size: 'A4',\n    margins: { top: 30, bottom: 30, left: 40, right: 40 },\n    autoFirstPage: true,\n    bufferPages: true,\n  });\n\n  const formattedDate = new Date(exercise.date + 'T00:00:00').toLocaleDateString('de-AT', {\n    weekday: 'long', day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric',\n  });\n  renderHeader(doc, 'Auswertung', `${exercise.name} — ${formattedDate}`);\n  renderSummaryCards(doc, stats);\n  renderPerRepeater(doc, stats.perRepeater);\n\n  // Visual section\n  if (stats.blStats.length > 0) {\n    doc.addPage();\n    renderAustriaMap(doc, stats.blStats);\n  }\n\n  const chartData = stats.bezirkStats.length > 0\n    ? stats.bezirkStats.map(bz => ({ label: `${bz.bundesland} ${bz.bezirk_code}`, participants: bz.participants, reports: bz.reports }))\n    : stats.blStats.map(bl => ({ label: bl.bundesland, participants: bl.participants, reports: bl.reports }));\n\n  if (chartData.length > 0) {\n    renderBarChart(doc, chartData);\n    renderPieChart(doc, chartData.map(d => ({ label: d.label, value: d.reports })));\n  }\n\n  // Tables\n  if (stats.bezirkStats.length > 0) {\n    doc.addPage();\n    renderBezirkTable(doc, stats.bezirkStats, stats);\n  } else if (stats.blStats.length > 0) {\n    doc.addPage();\n    renderBlTable(doc, stats.blStats, stats);\n  }\n\n  // Participants\n  doc.addPage();\n  renderParticipants(doc, stats.participants);\n\n  renderFooter(doc);\n  doc.end();\n  return doc;\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/routes/pdf.ts",
  "content": "import { Router } from 'express';\nimport { getDb } from '../db/database.js';\nimport { generateAggregatedPdf, generateExercisePdf } from '../services/pdf.js';\n\nexport const pdfRouter = Router();\n\n// GET /api/v1/pdf/reports?from=...&to=...&types=...\n// Aggregated PDF across a date range\npdfRouter.get('/reports', (req, res) => {\n  const { from, to, types } = req.query;\n  if (!from || !to) {\n    res.status(400).json({ error: 'from und to Parameter erforderlich (YYYY-MM-DD)' });\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const db = getDb();\n\n  let typeFilter = '';\n  const queryParams: any[] = [from, to];\n  if (types && typeof types === 'string' && types.trim()) {\n    const typeList = types.split(',').map(t => t.trim()).filter(Boolean);\n    if (typeList.length > 0) {\n      typeFilter = ` AND e.exercise_type IN (${typeList.map(() => '?').join(',')})`;\n      queryParams.push(...typeList);\n    }\n  }\n\n  const exercises = db.prepare(\n    `SELECT e.id, e.date, e.name, e.exercise_type,\n      (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_op_marker = 0) as participant_count,\n      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_op_marker = 0 AND readability IS NOT NULL) as report_count\n    FROM exercises e\n    WHERE e.date >= ? AND e.date <= ?${typeFilter}\n    ORDER BY e.date ASC`\n  ).all(...queryParams) as any[];\n\n  if (exercises.length === 0) {\n    res.status(404).json({ error: 'Keine Übungen im gewählten Zeitraum' });\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const ids = exercises.map((e: any) => e.id);\n  const placeholders = ids.map(() => '?').join(',');\n\n  const totalParticipants = (db.prepare(\n    `SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) as c FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND is_op_marker = 0`\n  ).get(...ids) as any)?.c || 0;\n\n  const totalReports = (db.prepare(\n    `SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND is_op_marker = 0 AND readability IS NOT NULL`\n  ).get(...ids) as any)?.c || 0;\n\n  const perRepeater = db.prepare(\n    `SELECT r.short_name, COUNT(*) as count\n    FROM signal_reports sr\n    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id\n    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND sr.is_op_marker = 0 AND sr.readability IS NOT NULL\n    GROUP BY sr.repeater_id\n    ORDER BY count DESC`\n  ).all(...ids) as any[];\n\n  const bezirkStats = db.prepare(\n    `SELECT bl.name as bundesland, bl.code as bundesland_code, bl.sort_order as bl_sort,\n      bz.code as bezirk_code, bz.name as bezirk_name, bz.is_capital,\n      COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,\n      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports\n    FROM signal_reports sr\n    JOIN bezirke bz ON bz.code = sr.bezirk_code\n    JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = bz.bundesland_code\n    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND sr.is_op_marker = 0\n    GROUP BY bz.code\n    ORDER BY bl.sort_order, bz.code`\n  ).all(...ids) as any[];\n\n  const blStats = db.prepare(\n    `SELECT\n      COALESCE(bl.name, 'Sonstige') as bundesland,\n      o.bundesland_code,\n      COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,\n      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports\n    FROM signal_reports sr\n    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id\n    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code\n    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND sr.is_op_marker = 0\n    GROUP BY o.bundesland_code\n    ORDER BY o.bundesland_code`\n  ).all(...ids) as any[];\n\n  const participants = db.prepare(\n    `SELECT o.callsign, o.name, o.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code,\n      bl.name as bundesland_name,\n      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as report_count\n    FROM signal_reports sr\n    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id\n    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code\n    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND sr.is_op_marker = 0\n    GROUP BY sr.operator_id\n    ORDER BY bl.sort_order, o.bezirk_code, o.callsign`\n  ).all(...ids) as any[];\n\n  const stats = { totalParticipants, totalReports, perRepeater, bezirkStats, blStats, participants };\n\n  const pdfDoc = generateAggregatedPdf(exercises, stats, from as string, to as string);\n\n  const filename = `BOS-ARSA_Auswertung_${from}_${to}.pdf`;\n  res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/pdf');\n  res.setHeader('Content-Disposition', `attachment; filename=\"${filename}\"`);\n  pdfDoc.pipe(res);\n});\n\n// GET /api/v1/pdf/exercises/:id\n// Single exercise PDF\npdfRouter.get('/exercises/:id', (req, res) => {\n  const db = getDb();\n  const eid = req.params.id;\n\n  const exercise = db.prepare('SELECT id, date, name, exercise_type FROM exercises WHERE id = ?').get(eid) as any;\n  if (!exercise) {\n    res.status(404).json({ error: 'Übung nicht gefunden' });\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const totalParticipants = (db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) as c FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = ? AND is_op_marker = 0').get(eid) as any)?.c || 0;\n  const totalReports = (db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = ? AND is_op_marker = 0 AND readability IS NOT NULL').get(eid) as any)?.c || 0;\n\n  const perRepeater = db.prepare(`\n    SELECT r.short_name, COUNT(*) as count\n    FROM signal_reports sr\n    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id\n    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0 AND sr.readability IS NOT NULL\n    GROUP BY sr.repeater_id\n    ORDER BY r.sort_order\n  `).all(eid) as any[];\n\n  const bezirkStats = db.prepare(`\n    SELECT bl.name as bundesland, bl.code as bundesland_code, bl.sort_order as bl_sort,\n      bz.code as bezirk_code, bz.name as bezirk_name, bz.is_capital,\n      COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,\n      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports\n    FROM signal_reports sr\n    JOIN bezirke bz ON bz.code = sr.bezirk_code\n    JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = bz.bundesland_code\n    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0\n    GROUP BY bz.code\n    ORDER BY bl.sort_order, bz.code\n  `).all(eid) as any[];\n\n  const blStats = db.prepare(`\n    SELECT\n      COALESCE(bl.name, 'Sonstige') as bundesland,\n      o.bundesland_code,\n      COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,\n      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports\n    FROM signal_reports sr\n    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id\n    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code\n    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0\n    GROUP BY o.bundesland_code\n    ORDER BY o.bundesland_code\n  `).all(eid) as any[];\n\n  const participants = db.prepare(`\n    SELECT o.callsign, o.name, o.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code,\n      bl.name as bundesland_name,\n      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as report_count\n    FROM signal_reports sr\n    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id\n    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code\n    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0\n    GROUP BY sr.operator_id\n    ORDER BY bl.sort_order, o.bezirk_code, o.callsign\n  `).all(eid) as any[];\n\n  const stats = { totalParticipants, totalReports, perRepeater, bezirkStats, blStats, participants };\n\n  const pdfDoc = generateExercisePdf(exercise, stats);\n\n  const filename = `BOS-ARSA_Auswertung_${exercise.date}.pdf`;\n  res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/pdf');\n  res.setHeader('Content-Disposition', `attachment; filename=\"${filename}\"`);\n  pdfDoc.pipe(res);\n});\n"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/pages/AggregatedReportsPage.tsx",
  "old_string": "  const downloadFullPage = async () => {\n    if (!pageRef.current) return;\n    setExporting(true);\n    const header = createExportHeader();\n    const footer = createExportFooter();\n    pageRef.current.style.padding = '24px';\n    pageRef.current.insertBefore(header, pageRef.current.firstChild);\n    pageRef.current.appendChild(footer);\n    try {\n      const url = await toPng(pageRef.current, {\n        backgroundColor: '#f3f4f6',\n        pixelRatio: 1,\n        filter: (node) => {\n          if (node instanceof HTMLElement && node.dataset.noExport === 'true') return false;\n          return true;\n        },\n      });\n      const link = document.createElement('a');\n      link.download = `BOS-ARSA_Auswertung_${from}_${to}.png`;\n      link.href = url;\n      link.click();\n    } catch {} finally {\n      header.remove();\n      footer.remove();\n      if (pageRef.current) pageRef.current.style.padding = '';\n      setExporting(false);\n    }\n  };",
  "new_string": "  const downloadFullPage = async () => {\n    setExporting(true);\n    try {\n      const token = localStorage.getItem('token');\n      const typesParam = selectedTypes.size > 0 ? `&types=${Array.from(selectedTypes).map(encodeURIComponent).join(',')}` : '';\n      const res = await fetch(`/api/v1/pdf/reports?from=${from}&to=${to}${typesParam}`, {\n        headers: token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {},\n      });\n      if (!res.ok) throw new Error();\n      const blob = await res.blob();\n      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);\n      const link = document.createElement('a');\n      link.href = url;\n      link.download = `BOS-ARSA_Auswertung_${from}_${to}.pdf`;\n      link.click();\n      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);\n    } catch {} finally {\n      setExporting(false);\n    }\n  };"
}

> AGENT

All tasks complete. Here's a summary of what was implemented:

## Summary

### Issue #8: Server-side PDF for "Download Auswertung"

**New files:**
- **`server/src/services/pdf.ts`** — PDFKit-based PDF generator with all render functions:
  - `renderHeader` — Navy blue header with "BOS-ARSA Log" branding
  - `renderSummaryCards` — Teilnehmer/Rapporte/Übungen stats
  - `renderPerRepeater` — Per-repeater report count grid
  - `renderExerciseTable` — Exercise list with date/name/participants/reports
  - `renderAustriaMap` — SVG paths drawn directly via PDFKit with heatmap colors
  - `renderBarChart` — Bar chart (Stationen + Rapporte) using rectangles
  - `renderPieChart` — Pie chart using arc segments
  - `renderBezirkTable` — Bezirk table grouped by Bundesland
  - `renderBlTable` — Bundesland-level fallback table
  - `renderParticipants` — Full participant list
  - `renderFooter` — "oeradio.at · bosarsa.oeradio.at"

- **`server/src/routes/pdf.ts`** — Two API endpoints:
  - `GET /api/v1/pdf/reports?from=...&to=...&types=...` — Aggregated PDF
  - `GET /api/v1/pdf/exercises/:id` — Single exercise PDF

**Modified files:**
- **`server/src/index.ts`** — Registered PDF route with `authMiddleware`
- **`server/package.json`** — Added `pdfkit` + `@types/pdfkit`
- **`client/src/pages/AggregatedReportsPage.tsx`** — `downloadFullPage()` now fetches server PDF instead of using `toPng()`
- **`client/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx`** — Same change, button label updated to "PDF — Download Auswertung"

**What still works unchanged:** Individual chart downloads (Karte, Balken, Torte) remain as PNG via `html-to-image`/`toPng()`.

     1→# BOS-ARSA Log — Sitzungsbericht 24.02.2026
     2→
     3→## Erledigte Aufgaben
     4→
     5→### #1 — Rapport soll verschiebbar innerhalb der Bezirke sein
     6→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/1
     7→- **Status:** Erledigt und deployed
     8→- **Umsetzung:**
     9→  - **Bezirk-Dropdown im Bearbeitungsmodus:** Beim Klicken auf einen Rapport erscheint ein Bezirk-Auswahlfeld neben Rapport, Notizen usw. Bezirk wechseln und mit OK bestätigen.
    10→  - **Drag & Drop:** Rapport-Zeilen können per Drag & Drop zwischen Bezirk-Abschnitten verschoben werden. Das Ziel wird blau hervorgehoben. Funktioniert in Frequenzen- und OE-Link-Modus.
    11→  - Dropdown funktioniert auf allen Geräten (inkl. Mobilgeräte), Drag & Drop auf Desktop.
    12→  - Kein Backend-Änderung nötig — der PATCH-Endpunkt unterstützte `bezirk_code` bereits.
    13→
    14→### Datenbank-Backup
    15→- Produktions-Backup manuell über das bestehende Backup-Skript (`backup-bosarsalog.sh`) ausgelöst und auf Synology NAS verifiziert.
    16→- Zusätzliches lokales Backup in `backups/bosarsalog-backup-2026-02-24.db` (1,6 MB, Integritätsprüfung bestanden).
    17→
    18→### Repeater-Analyse
    19→- Detaillierte Analyse der gemeldeten Verwirrung bei Schöckl 2m/70cm und Lachtal im Setup durchgeführt.
    20→- **Ergebnis:** Kein Datenfehler — die Filterlogik (linked → OE-Link, non-linked → Frequenzen) funktioniert korrekt wie vorgesehen.
    21→
    22→### #2 — Option Bezirkskenner in Simplex Frequenzen anzuzeigen
    23→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/2
    24→- **Status:** Erledigt und deployed
    25→- **Umsetzung:**
    26→  - **Neuer Toggle pro Übung:** Admin-only Checkbox "Simplex Bezirke aktivieren" in der Übungs-Einrichtung (nach OE-Link-Toggle). Standardmäßig deaktiviert.
    27→  - **Schema-Migration:** Neue Spalte `simplex_bezirke INTEGER DEFAULT 0` in `exercises`-Tabelle (nicht-destruktiver ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN).
    28→  - **API:** GET/POST/PATCH Exercises-Routen unterstützen `simplex_bezirke`-Feld.
    29→  - **LandMode-Gruppierung:** Wenn aktiviert, zeigen Simplex-Frequenzkarten Bundesland-Unterüberschriften mit Bezirk-Zeilen (BezirkRow) statt flacher Liste (FlatReportRow).
    30→  - Bezirke werden aus den gewählten Bundesländern übernommen.
    31→  - **BL-Auswahl konfigurierbar:** Unterhalb des Simplex-Bezirke-Toggles erscheint ein kompaktes Bundesländer-Grid. Admin kann pro Übung auswählen, welche BLs in der Simplex-Gruppierung angezeigt werden. Beim erstmaligen Aktivieren werden alle 9 BLs vorausgewählt. Schema: neue Spalte `simplex_bl_codes TEXT` (komma-separiert, NULL = alle).
    32→  - Drag & Drop zwischen Bezirken funktioniert im gruppierten Modus.
    33→  - Nicht zugeordnete Rapporte erscheinen im "Sonstige"-Bereich.
    34→  - Toggle aus → Rapporte werden wieder flach angezeigt (Daten bleiben erhalten).
    35→  - **Geänderte Dateien:** `schema.ts`, `exercises.ts`, `ExerciseSetupPage.tsx`, `ExercisePage.tsx`, `LandMode.tsx`
    36→
    37→### #4 — Eingabe vom OP Rufzeichen wird nicht in Echtzeit übernommen
    38→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/4
    39→- **Status:** Erledigt und deployed
    40→- **Umsetzung:**
    41→  - **Neues WebSocket-Event `op_callsign_updated`:** Server empfängt das Event und broadcastet es an alle anderen Clients im selben Exercise-Room.
    42→  - **LandMode (Frequenzen-Modus):** Nach dem Speichern eines OP-Rufzeichens wird `op_callsign_updated` emittiert. Eingehende Events aktualisieren `opCallsigns`-State sofort.
    43→  - **BundMode (OE-Link-Modus):** Gleicher Pattern — nach API-Call wird emittiert. Eingehende Events mappen `repeater_id` zurück auf `blCode` via `blRepSelection` und aktualisieren `blOpCallsigns`.
    44→  - Cleanup: Socket-Listener werden beim Unmount/Re-Mount korrekt entfernt.
    45→  - **Geänderte Dateien:** `socket.ts` (Server), `LandMode.tsx`, `BundMode.tsx`
    46→
    47→---
    48→
    49→## Offene Issues
    50→
    51→### #3 — Bei den Repeatern bitte „eigener Repeater" dazufügen — Klärung
    52→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/3
    53→- Klärungsbedarf: benutzerdefinierte Repeater können bereits über Einrichtung hinzugefügt werden. Zu klären, ob weitere Funktionalität gewünscht.
    54→
    55→### #5 — Eventuell mehrere OPs bei Simplex
    56→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/5
    57→- **Status:** Geschlossen — bereits möglich
    58→- Mehrere OPs können kommagetrennt im OP-Feld eingetragen werden, z.B. `OE1OP1, OE2OP2`. Das Feld ist ein Freitext-Input ohne Beschränkung auf ein einzelnes Rufzeichen.
    59→
    60→### #6 — Rufzeichen doppelt eingeben führt zu Problemen
    61→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/6
    62→- **Status:** Erledigt
    63→- **Problem:** Wenn ein Rufzeichen doppelt eingegeben wird (gleicher Operator + gleicher Repeater), greift die UNIQUE-Constraint der DB und der Server gibt 409 zurück. Der Client versuchte den existierenden Report per `reports.find()` im lokalen State zu finden, um ihn zu PATCHen. Bei veraltetem State passierte **gar nichts** — kein Feedback, kein Update.
    64→- **Fix:** Server liefert bei 409 den existierenden Report als `existing_report` im Response-Body mit. Client nutzt diesen direkt statt im lokalen State zu suchen. `apiFetch` hängt den vollen Response-Body als `error.data` am Error-Objekt an.
    65→- **Geänderte Dateien:** `exercises.ts` (Server), `api.ts`, `LandMode.tsx`, `BundMode.tsx`
    66→

Dateiname: `.pdf` statt `.png`.\n  - Einzelne Chart-Downloads (Karte, Balken, Torte) bleiben als PNG via `html-to-image` — **kein Funktionsverlust**.\n  - PDF: A4 Querformat, automatische Seitenumbrüche.\n- **Geänderte Dateien:** `server/src/services/pdf.ts` (NEU), `server/src/routes/pdf.ts` (NEU), `server/src/index.ts`, `server/package.json`, `client/src/pages/AggregatedReportsPage.tsx`, `client/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx`\n\n### #6 — Rufzeichen doppelt eingeben führt zu Problemen"

österreich karte ist schwarz.

> AGENT

Das Problem ist, dass PDFKit keine CSS `rgb(r,g,b)` Farbwerte versteht — es braucht Hex-Farben. Außerdem muss `fillAndStroke` statt `fill().stroke()` verwendet werden.

> AGENT

Zwei Probleme gefunden:
1. `getHeatColor` gibt `rgb(r,g,b)` zurück — PDFKit versteht nur Hex
2. `.fill().stroke()` funktioniert nicht — braucht `fillAndStroke()`

> TOOL

id: toolu_01Lv9n1bfiimF6QKyQPTxQmg
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/services/pdf.ts",
  "old_string": "function getHeatColor(value: number, max: number): string {\n  if (max === 0 || value === 0) return '#e5e7eb';\n  const t = value / max;\n  const r = Math.round(194 + (30 - 194) * t);\n  const g = Math.round(213 + (58 - 213) * t);\n  const b = Math.round(236 + (95 - 236) * t);\n  return `rgb(${r},${g},${b})`;\n}",
  "new_string": "function getHeatColor(value: number, max: number): string {\n  if (max === 0 || value === 0) return '#e5e7eb';\n  const t = value / max;\n  const r = Math.round(194 + (30 - 194) * t);\n  const g = Math.round(213 + (58 - 213) * t);\n  const b = Math.round(236 + (95 - 236) * t);\n  return '#' + [r, g, b].map(c => c.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');\n}"
}

> AGENT

Das Hauptproblem bei allen Tabellen: PDFKit's `doc.text()` bewegt `doc.y` nach jedem Aufruf. Bei mehreren Spalten in einer Zeile verschiebt sich die Y-Position mit jedem `text()`-Aufruf. Ich muss die Y-Position vor jeder Zeile speichern und mit `{ lineBreak: false }` arbeiten.

Ich schreibe die gesamten Tabellen-Funktionen und den Header neu, damit alles sauber funktioniert.

   122→function renderHeader(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, title: string, subtitle: string) {
   123→  const pageW = doc.page.width;
   124→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   125→  doc.save();
   126→  doc.rect(m.left, m.top, pageW - m.left - m.right, 50).fill(NAVY);
   127→  doc.fontSize(18).fillColor(WHITE).text('BOS-ARSA Log', m.left + 15, m.top + 10, { continued: false });
   128→  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor('#93c5fd').text('Im Sinne der Sicherheit', m.left + 15, m.top + 32);
   129→  doc.restore();
   130→  doc.y = m.top + 60;
   131→  doc.fontSize(14).fillColor(NAVY).text(title, m.left, doc.y);
   132→  doc.fontSize(9).fillColor(GRAY_500).text(subtitle, m.left, doc.y);
   133→  doc.moveDown(0.8);
   134→}
   135→
   136→function renderSummaryCards(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, stats: PdfStats, exerciseCount?: number) {
   137→  ensureSpace(doc, 50);
   138→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   139→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   140→  const cols = exerciseCount !== undefined ? 4 : 2;
   141→  const cardW = usable / cols - 8;
   142→  const startX = m.left;
   143→  const startY = doc.y;
   144→
   145→  const cards: { value: string | number; label: string }[] = [
   146→    { value: stats.totalParticipants, label: 'Teilnehmer' },
   147→    { value: stats.totalReports, label: 'Rapporte' },
   148→  ];
   149→  if (exerciseCount !== undefined) {
   150→    cards.push({ value: exerciseCount, label: 'Übungen' });
   151→    const avg = exerciseCount > 0 ? Math.round(stats.totalParticipants / exerciseCount * 10) / 10 : 0;
   152→    cards.push({ value: avg, label: 'Ø Teiln./Übung' });
   153→  }
   154→
   155→  cards.forEach((c, i) => {
   156→    const x = startX + i * (cardW + 8);
   157→    doc.save();
   158→    doc.roundedRect(x, startY, cardW, 40, 4).fill(GRAY_50);
   159→    doc.fontSize(18).fillColor(NAVY).text(String(c.value), x, startY + 5, { width: cardW, align: 'center' });
   160→    doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(GRAY_500).text(c.label, x, startY + 27, { width: cardW, align: 'center' });
   161→    doc.restore();
   162→  });
   163→  doc.y = startY + 48;
   164→}
   165→
   166→function renderPerRepeater(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, perRepeater: PerRepeater[]) {
   167→  if (perRepeater.length === 0) return;

   169→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   170→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   171→  const cols = Math.min(perRepeater.length, 6);
   172→  const cardW = usable / cols - 6;
   173→  const startX = m.left;
   174→  const startY = doc.y;
   175→
   176→  perRepeater.slice(0, 6).forEach((r, i) => {

   181→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500).text(r.short_name, x, startY + 17, { width: cardW, align: 'center' });
   182→    doc.restore();
   183→  });
   184→  doc.y = startY + 36;
   185→}
