   589→// ── Public API ────────────────────────────────────────────
   590→
   591→export function generateAggregatedPdf(
   592→  exercises: ExerciseSummary[],
   593→  stats: PdfStats,
   594→  from: string,
   595→  to: string,
   596→): PDFKit.PDFDocument {
   597→  const doc = new PDFDocument({
   598→    layout: 'landscape',
   599→    size: 'A4',
   600→    margins: { top: 30, bottom: 30, left: 40, right: 40 },
   601→    autoFirstPage: true,
   602→    bufferPages: true,
   603→  });
   604→
   605→  renderHeader(doc, 'Auswertung', `${formatDateDE(from)} – ${formatDateDE(to)}  ·  ${exercises.length} Übung${exercises.length !== 1 ? 'en' : ''}`);
   606→  renderSummaryCards(doc, stats, exercises.length);
   607→  renderPerRepeater(doc, stats.perRepeater);
   608→  renderExerciseTable(doc, exercises);
   609→
   610→  // Visual section: map + charts
   611→  if (stats.blStats.length > 0) {
   612→    doc.addPage();
   613→    renderAustriaMap(doc, stats.blStats);
   614→  }
   615→
   616→  const chartData = stats.bezirkStats.length > 0
   617→    ? stats.bezirkStats.map(bz => ({ label: `${bz.bundesland} ${bz.bezirk_code}`, participants: bz.participants, reports: bz.reports }))
   618→    : stats.blStats.map(bl => ({ label: bl.bundesland, participants: bl.participants, reports: bl.reports }));
   619→
   620→  if (chartData.length > 0) {
   621→    renderBarChart(doc, chartData);
   622→    renderPieChart(doc, chartData.map(d => ({ label: d.label, value: d.reports })));
   623→  }
   624→
   625→  // Tables
   626→  if (stats.bezirkStats.length > 0) {
   627→    doc.addPage();
   628→    renderBezirkTable(doc, stats.bezirkStats, stats);
   629→  } else if (stats.blStats.length > 0) {
   630→    doc.addPage();
   631→    renderBlTable(doc, stats.blStats, stats);
   632→  }
   633→
   634→  // Participants
   635→  doc.addPage();
   636→  renderParticipants(doc, stats.participants);
   637→
   638→  renderFooter(doc);
   639→  doc.end();
   640→  return doc;
   641→}

   644→  exercise: { name: string; date: string; exercise_type?: string | null },
   645→  stats: PdfStats,
   646→): PDFKit.PDFDocument {
   647→  const doc = new PDFDocument({
   648→    layout: 'landscape',
   649→    size: 'A4',
   650→    margins: { top: 30, bottom: 30, left: 40, right: 40 },
   651→    autoFirstPage: true,
   652→    bufferPages: true,
   653→  });
   654→
   655→  const formattedDate = new Date(exercise.date + 'T00:00:00').toLocaleDateString('de-AT', {
   656→    weekday: 'long', day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric',
   657→  });
   658→  renderHeader(doc, 'Auswertung', `${exercise.name} — ${formattedDate}`);
   659→  renderSummaryCards(doc, stats);
   660→  renderPerRepeater(doc, stats.perRepeater);
   661→
   662→  // Visual section
   663→  if (stats.blStats.length > 0) {
   664→    doc.addPage();
   665→    renderAustriaMap(doc, stats.blStats);
   666→  }
   667→
   668→  const chartData = stats.bezirkStats.length > 0
   669→    ? stats.bezirkStats.map(bz => ({ label: `${bz.bundesland} ${bz.bezirk_code}`, participants: bz.participants, reports: bz.reports }))
   670→    : stats.blStats.map(bl => ({ label: bl.bundesland, participants: bl.participants, reports: bl.reports }));
   671→
   672→  if (chartData.length > 0) {
   673→    renderBarChart(doc, chartData);
   674→    renderPieChart(doc, chartData.map(d => ({ label: d.label, value: d.reports })));
   675→  }
   676→
   677→  // Tables
   678→  if (stats.bezirkStats.length > 0) {
   679→    doc.addPage();
   680→    renderBezirkTable(doc, stats.bezirkStats, stats);
   681→  } else if (stats.blStats.length > 0) {
   682→    doc.addPage();
   683→    renderBlTable(doc, stats.blStats, stats);
   684→  }
   685→
   686→  // Participants
   687→  doc.addPage();
   688→  renderParticipants(doc, stats.participants);
   689→
   690→  renderFooter(doc);
   691→  doc.end();
   692→  return doc;
   693→}
   694→

'OE9',\n};\n\n// ── Helper functions ─────────────────────────────────────\n\nfunction getHeatColor(value: number, max: number): string {\n  if (max === 0 || value === 0) return '#e5e7eb';\n  const t = value / max;\n  const r = Math.round(194 + (30 - 194) * t);\n  const g = Math.round(213 + (58 - 213) * t);\n  const b = Math.round(236 + (95 - 236) * t);\n  return '#' + [r, g, b].map(c => c.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');\n}\n\nfunction formatDateDE(d: string): string {\n  const date = new Date(d + 'T00:00:00');\n  return date.toLocaleDateString('de-AT', { day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric' });\n}\n\nfunction ensureSpace(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, needed: number) {\n  const bottom = doc.page.height - doc.page.margins.bottom;\n  if (doc.y + needed > bottom) {\n    doc.addPage();\n  }\n}\n\n/** Write text at fixed position without moving doc.y */\nfunction textAt(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, str: string, x: number, y: number, opts?: PDFKit.Mixins.TextOptions) {\n  doc.text(str, x, y, { lineBreak: false, ...opts });\n}\n\n// ── Render functions ─────────────────────────────────────\n\nfunction renderHeader(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, title: string, subtitle: string) {\n  const pageW = doc.page.width;\n  const m = doc.page.margins;\n  const barW = pageW - m.left - m.right;\n\n  doc.save();\n  doc.rect(m.left, m.top, barW, 50).fill(NAVY);\n  doc.fontSize(18).fillColor(WHITE);\n  textAt(doc, 'BOS-ARSA Log', m.left + 15, m.top + 10);\n  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor('#93c5fd');\n  textAt(doc, 'Im Sinne der Sicherheit', m.left + 15, m.top + 32);\n  doc.restore();\n\n  doc.y = m.top + 60;\n  doc.fontSize(14).fillColor(NAVY);\n  textAt(doc, title, m.left, doc.y);\n  doc.y += 18;\n  doc.fontSize(9).fillColor(GRAY_500);\n  textAt(doc, subtitle, m.left, doc.y);\n  doc.y += 16;\n}\n\nfunction renderSummaryCards(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, stats: PdfStats, exerciseCount?: number) {\n  ensureSpace(doc, 50);\n  const m = doc.page.margins;\n  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;\n  const cols = exerciseCount !== undefined ? 4 : 2;\n  const cardW = usable / cols - 8;\n  const startY = doc.y;\n\n  const cards: { value: string | number; label: string }[] = [\n    { value: stats.totalParticipants, label: 'Teilnehmer' },\n    { value: stats.totalReports, label: 'Rapporte' },\n  ];\n  if (exerciseCount !== undefined) {\n    cards.push({ value: exerciseCount, label: 'Übungen' });\n    const avg = exerciseCount > 0 ? Math.round(stats.totalParticipants / exerciseCount * 10) / 10 : 0;\n    cards.push({ value: avg, label: 'Ø Teiln./Übung' });\n  }\n\n  cards.forEach((c, i) => {\n    const x = m.left + i * (cardW + 8);\n    doc.save();\n    doc.roundedRect(x, startY, cardW, 40, 4).fill(GRAY_50);\n    doc.fontSize(18).fillColor(NAVY);\n    textAt(doc, String(c.value), x, startY + 5, { width: cardW, align: 'center' });\n    doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(GRAY_500);\n    textAt(doc, c.label, x, startY + 27, { width: cardW, align: 'center' });\n    doc.restore();\n  });\n  doc.y = startY + 48;\n}\n\nfunction renderPerRepeater(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, perRepeater: PerRepeater[]) {\n  if (perRepeater.length === 0) return;\n  ensureSpace(doc, 35);\n  const m = doc.page.margins;\n  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;\n  const cols = Math.min(perRepeater.length, 6);\n  const cardW = usable / cols - 6;\n  const startY = doc.y;\n\n  perRepeater.slice(0, 6).forEach((r, i) => {\n    const x = m.left + i * (cardW + 6);\n    doc.save();\n    doc.roundedRect(x, startY, cardW, 28, 3).fill(GRAY_50);\n    doc.fontSize(12).fillColor(GRAY_500);\n    textAt(doc, String(r.count), x, startY + 2, { width: cardW, align: 'center' });\n    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);\n    textAt(doc, r.short_name, x, startY + 17, { width: cardW, align: 'center' });\n    doc.restore();\n  });\n  doc.y = startY + 36;\n}\n\nfunction renderExerciseTable(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, exercises: ExerciseSummary[]) {\n  if (exercises.length === 0) return;\n  ensureSpace(doc, 60);\n  const m = doc.page.margins;\n  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;\n\n  // Section header\n  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 18).fill(NAVY);\n  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor(WHITE);\n  textAt(doc, `Übungen (${exercises.length})`, m.left + 8, doc.y + 4);\n  doc.y += 20;\n\n  // Column positions\n  const colDate = m.left + 5;\n  const colName = m.left + 80;\n  const colPart = m.left + usable - 90;\n  const colRap = m.left + usable - 40;\n\n  // Column headers\n  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 14).fill(GRAY_100);\n  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);\n  const hy = doc.y + 4;\n  textAt(doc, 'DATUM', colDate, hy);\n  textAt(doc, 'NAME', colName, hy);\n  textAt(doc, 'TEILN.', colPart, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n  textAt(doc, 'RAPP.', colRap, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n  doc.y += 16;\n\n  exercises.forEach((ex, i) => {\n    ensureSpace(doc, 14);\n    const rowY = doc.y;\n    if (i % 2 === 0) {\n      doc.save();\n      doc.rect(m.left, rowY, usable, 12).fill(GRAY_50);\n      doc.restore();\n    }\n    doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);\n    textAt(doc, formatDateDE(ex.date), colDate, rowY + 2);\n    textAt(doc, ex.name || '', colName, rowY + 2, { width: colPart - colName - 10 });\n    textAt(doc, String(ex.participant_count), colPart, rowY + 2, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n    textAt(doc, String(ex.report_count), colRap, rowY + 2, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n    doc.y = rowY + 13;\n  });\n  doc.y += 4;\n}\n\nfunction renderAustriaMap(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, blStats: BlStat[]) {\n  if (blStats.length === 0) return;\n  ensureSpace(doc, 200);\n\n  const m = doc.page.margins;\n  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;\n  const mapW = Math.min(usable, 400);\n  const mapH = mapW * 0.5;\n  const offsetX = m.left + (usable - mapW) / 2;\n\n  doc.fontSize(9).fillColor(NAVY);\n  textAt(doc, 'Teilnehmer nach Bundesland', m.left, doc.y);\n  doc.y += 14;\n  const mapStartY = doc.y;\n\n  const dataMap = new Map(blStats.map(bl => [bl.bundesland_code, bl]));\n  const maxP = Math.max(...blStats.map(bl => bl.participants), 1);\n\n  const svgW = 620;\n  const svgH = 300;\n  const scale = Math.min(mapW / svgW, mapH / svgH);\n\n  for (const [code, pathData] of Object.entries(BL_PATHS)) {\n    const bl = dataMap.get(code);\n    const participants = bl?.participants ?? 0;\n    const fillColor = getHeatColor(participants, maxP);\n\n    doc.save();\n    doc.translate(offsetX, mapStartY);\n    doc.scale(scale);\n    doc.path(pathData.d).lineWidth(2 / scale).fillAndStroke(fillColor, WHITE);\n    doc.restore();\n\n    // Label\n    const lx = offsetX + pathData.labelX * scale;\n    const ly = mapStartY + pathData.labelY * scale;\n    doc.save();\n    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(NAVY);\n    textAt(doc, BL_SHORT[code] || code, lx - 15, ly - 6, { width: 30, align: 'center' });\n    doc.fontSize(5).fillColor(NAVY);\n    textAt(doc, String(participants), lx - 15, ly + 1, { width: 30, align: 'center' });\n    doc.restore();\n  }\n\n  doc.y = mapStartY + mapH + 10;\n}\n\nfunction renderBarChart(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, chartData: { label: string; participants: number; reports: number }[]) {\n  if (chartData.length === 0) return;\n  ensureSpace(doc, 180);\n\n  const m = doc.page.margins;\n  const chartW = Math.min(doc.page.width - m.left - m.right, 500);\n  const chartH = 140;\n  const startX = m.left;\n\n  doc.fontSize(9).fillColor(NAVY);\n  textAt(doc, 'Stationen & Rapporte', m.left, doc.y);\n  doc.y += 15;\n  const startY = doc.y;\n\n  const maxVal = Math.max(...chartData.map(d => Math.max(d.participants, d.reports)), 1);\n  const barAreaW = chartW - 30;\n  const groupW = barAreaW / chartData.length;\n  const barW = Math.min(groupW * 0.35, 20);\n\n  // Y-axis gridlines\n  for (let i = 0; i <= 4; i++) {\n    const y = startY + chartH - (chartH * i / 4);\n    const val = Math.round(maxVal * i / 4);\n    doc.save();\n    doc.strokeColor(GRAY_200).lineWidth(0.5).moveTo(startX + 25, y).lineTo(startX + chartW, y).stroke();\n    doc.fontSize(5).fillColor(GRAY_500);\n    textAt(doc, String(val), startX, y - 3, { width: 22, align: 'right' });\n    doc.restore();\n  }\n\n  // Bars\n  chartData.forEach((d, i) => {\n    const groupX = startX + 30 + i * groupW;\n    const pH = (d.participants / maxVal) * chartH;\n    doc.rect(groupX, startY + chartH - pH, barW, pH).fill(NAVY);\n    const rH = (d.reports / maxVal) * chartH;\n    doc.rect(groupX + barW + 2, startY + chartH - rH, barW, rH).fill(RED);\n    doc.save();\n    doc.fontSize(4).fillColor(GRAY_500);\n    textAt(doc, d.label, groupX - 2, startY + chartH + 3, { width: groupW });\n    doc.restore();\n  });\n\n  // Legend\n  const legendY = startY + chartH + 14;\n  doc.rect(startX + 30, legendY, 8, 6).fill(NAVY);\n  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);\n  textAt(doc, 'Stationen', startX + 42, legendY);\n  doc.rect(startX + 90, legendY, 8, 6).fill(RED);\n  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);\n  textAt(doc, 'Rapporte', startX + 102, legendY);\n  doc.y = legendY + 14;\n}\n\nfunction renderPieChart(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, chartData: { label: string; value: number }[]) {\n  const filtered = chartData.filter(d => d.value > 0);\n  if (filtered.length === 0) return;\n  ensureSpace(doc, 180);\n\n  const m = doc.page.margins;\n  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;\n\n  doc.fontSize(9).fillColor(NAVY);\n  textAt(doc, 'Rapporte Verteilung', m.left, doc.y);\n  doc.y += 12;\n\n  const cx = m.left + 100;\n  const cy = doc.y + 70;\n  const r = 60;\n  const total = filtered.reduce((s, d) => s + d.value, 0);\n  let startAngle = -Math.PI / 2;\n\n  filtered.forEach((d, i) => {\n    const sliceAngle = (d.value / total) * 2 * Math.PI;\n    const endAngle = startAngle + sliceAngle;\n    const color = PIE_COLORS[i % PIE_COLORS.length];\n\n    const x1 = cx + r * Math.cos(startAngle);\n    const y1 = cy + r * Math.sin(startAngle);\n    const x2 = cx + r * Math.cos(endAngle);\n    const y2 = cy + r * Math.sin(endAngle);\n    const largeArc = sliceAngle > Math.PI ? 1 : 0;\n\n    doc.path(`M ${cx} ${cy} L ${x1} ${y1} A ${r} ${r} 0 ${largeArc} 1 ${x2} ${y2} Z`).fill(color);\n    startAngle = endAngle;\n  });\n\n  // Legend\n  const legendX = cx + r + 30;\n  filtered.forEach((d, i) => {\n    const ly = cy - r + i * 12;\n    if (ly > doc.page.height - m.bottom) return;\n    const color = PIE_COLORS[i % PIE_COLORS.length];\n    doc.rect(legendX, ly, 8, 8).fill(color);\n    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);\n    textAt(doc, `${d.label} (${d.value})`, legendX + 12, ly + 1, { width: usable - legendX + m.left - 12 });\n  });\n\n  doc.y = cy + r + 15;\n}\n\nfunction renderBezirkTable(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, bezirkStats: BezirkStat[], stats: PdfStats) {\n  if (bezirkStats.length === 0) return;\n  ensureSpace(doc, 60);\n\n  const m = doc.page.margins;\n  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;\n  const colName = m.left + 5;\n  const colPart = m.left + usable - 90;\n  const colRap = m.left + usable - 40;\n\n  // Section header\n  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 18).fill(NAVY);\n  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor(WHITE);\n  textAt(doc, 'Nebenstationen nach Bezirk', m.left + 8, doc.y + 4);\n  doc.y += 20;\n\n  // Column headers\n  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 14).fill(GRAY_100);\n  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);\n  const hy = doc.y + 4;\n  textAt(doc, 'BUNDESLAND / BEZIRK', colName, hy);\n  textAt(doc, 'TEILN.', colPart, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n  textAt(doc, 'RAPP.', colRap, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n  doc.y += 16;\n\n  // Group by Bundesland\n  const byBl: Record<string, { name: string; bezirke: BezirkStat[]; totalP: number; totalR: number }> = {};\n  for (const bz of bezirkStats) {\n    if (!byBl[bz.bundesland_code]) {\n      byBl[bz.bundesland_code] = { name: bz.bundesland, bezirke: [], totalP: 0, totalR: 0 };\n    }\n    byBl[bz.bundesland_code].bezirke.push(bz);\n    byBl[bz.bundesland_code].totalP += bz.participants;\n    byBl[bz.bundesland_code].totalR += bz.reports;\n  }\n\n  for (const [, bl] of Object.entries(byBl)) {\n    ensureSpace(doc, 14);\n    const rowY = doc.y;\n    doc.rect(m.left, rowY, usable, 13).fill(GRAY_100);\n    doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);\n    textAt(doc, bl.name, colName, rowY + 3);\n    textAt(doc, String(bl.totalP), colPart, rowY + 3, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n    textAt(doc, String(bl.totalR), colRap, rowY + 3, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n    doc.y = rowY + 14;\n\n    bl.bezirke.forEach(bz => {\n      ensureSpace(doc, 12);\n      const ry = doc.y;\n      doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_MED);\n      textAt(doc, `    ${bz.bezirk_code}  ${bz.bezirk_name}`, colName, ry + 1);\n      textAt(doc, String(bz.participants), colPart, ry + 1, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n      textAt(doc, String(bz.reports), colRap, ry + 1, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n      doc.y = ry + 11;\n    });\n  }\n\n  // Gesamt\n  ensureSpace(doc, 18);\n  const gy = doc.y;\n  doc.rect(m.left, gy, usable, 16).fill(NAVY);\n  doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(WHITE);\n  textAt(doc, 'Gesamt', colName, gy + 4);\n  textAt(doc, String(stats.totalParticipants), colPart, gy + 4, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n  textAt(doc, String(stats.totalReports), colRap, gy + 4, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n  doc.y = gy + 20;\n}\n\nfunction renderBlTable(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, blStats: BlStat[], stats: PdfStats) {\n  if (blStats.length === 0) return;\n  ensureSpace(doc, 60);\n\n  const m = doc.page.margins;\n  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;\n  const colName = m.left + 5;\n  const colPart = m.left + usable - 90;\n  const colRap = m.left + usable - 40;\n\n  // Section header\n  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 18).fill(NAVY);\n  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor(WHITE);\n  textAt(doc, 'Teilnehmer nach Bundesland / Land', m.left + 8, doc.y + 4);\n  doc.y += 20;\n\n  // Column headers\n  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 14).fill(GRAY_100);\n  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);\n  const hy = doc.y + 4;\n  textAt(doc, 'BUNDESLAND', colName, hy);\n  textAt(doc, 'TEILNEHMER', colPart, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n  textAt(doc, 'RAPPORTE', colRap, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n  doc.y += 16;\n\n  blStats.forEach((bl, i) => {\n    ensureSpace(doc, 12);\n    const rowY = doc.y;\n    if (i % 2 === 0) {\n      doc.save();\n      doc.rect(m.left, rowY, usable, 12).fill(GRAY_50);\n      doc.restore();\n    }\n    doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);\n    textAt(doc, bl.bundesland, colName, rowY + 2);\n    textAt(doc, String(bl.participants), colPart, rowY + 2, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n    textAt(doc, String(bl.reports), colRap, rowY + 2, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n    doc.y = rowY + 13;\n  });\n\n  // Gesamt\n  ensureSpace(doc, 18);\n  const gy = doc.y;\n  doc.rect(m.left, gy, usable, 16).fill(NAVY);\n  doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(WHITE);\n  textAt(doc, 'Gesamt', colName, gy + 4);\n  textAt(doc, String(stats.totalParticipants), colPart, gy + 4, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n  textAt(doc, String(stats.totalReports), colRap, gy + 4, { width: 40, align: 'right' });\n  doc.y = gy + 20;\n}\n\nfunction renderParticipants(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, participants: Participant[]) {\n  if (participants.length === 0) return;\n  ensureSpace(doc, 60);\n\n  const m = doc.page.margins;\n  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;\n\n  // Section header\n  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 18).fill(NAVY);\n  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor(WHITE);\n  textAt(doc, `Teilnehmer-Liste (${participants.length})`, m.left + 8, doc.y + 4);\n  doc.y += 20;\n\n  // Column positions\n  const colNum = m.left + 3;\n  const colCall = m.left + 25;\n  const colName = m.left + 90;\n  const colBz = m.left + usable - 135;\n  const colBl = m.left + usable - 90;\n  const colRap = m.left + usable - 35;\n\n  // Column headers\n  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 14).fill(GRAY_100);\n  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);\n  const hy = doc.y + 4;\n  textAt(doc, '#', colNum, hy);\n  textAt(doc, 'RUFZEICHEN', colCall, hy);\n  textAt(doc, 'NAME', colName, hy);\n  textAt(doc, 'BEZ.', colBz, hy);\n  textAt(doc, 'BUNDESLAND', colBl, hy);\n  textAt(doc, 'RAPP.', colRap, hy, { width: 35, align: 'right' });\n  doc.y += 16;\n\n  participants.forEach((p, i) => {\n    ensureSpace(doc, 11);\n    const rowY = doc.y;\n    if (i % 2 === 0) {\n      doc.save();\n      doc.rect(m.left, rowY, usable, 10).fill(GRAY_50);\n      doc.restore();\n    }\n    const ty = rowY + 2;\n    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);\n    textAt(doc, String(i + 1), colNum, ty);\n    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);\n    textAt(doc, p.callsign, colCall, ty);\n    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_MED);\n    textAt(doc, p.name || '', colName, ty, { width: colBz - colName - 5 });\n    textAt(doc, p.bezirk_code || '', colBz, ty);\n    textAt(doc, p.bundesland_name || '', colBl, ty, { width: colRap - colBl - 5 });\n    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);\n    textAt(doc, String(p.report_count), colRap, ty, { width: 35, align: 'right' });\n    doc.y = rowY + 11;\n  });\n  doc.y += 4;\n}\n\nfunction renderFooter(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument) {\n  const m = doc.page.margins;\n  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;\n  ensureSpace(doc, 25);\n  doc.strokeColor(GRAY_200).lineWidth(0.5).moveTo(m.left, doc.y).lineTo(m.left + usable, doc.y).stroke();\n  doc.y += 8;\n  doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(GRAY_500);\n  textAt(doc, 'oeradio.at  ·  bosarsa.oeradio.at', m.left, doc.y, { width: usable, align: 'center' });\n}\n\n// ── Public API ────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nexport function generateAggregatedPdf(\n  exercises: ExerciseSummary[],\n  stats: PdfStats,\n  from: string,\n  to: string,\n): PDFKit.PDFDocument {\n  const doc = new PDFDocument({\n    layout: 'landscape',\n    size: 'A4',\n    margins: { top: 30, bottom: 30, left: 40, right: 40 },\n    autoFirstPage: true,\n    bufferPages: true,\n  });\n\n  renderHeader(doc, 'Auswertung', `${formatDateDE(from)} – ${formatDateDE(to)}  ·  ${exercises.length} Übung${exercises.length !== 1 ? 'en' : ''}`);\n  renderSummaryCards(doc, stats, exercises.length);\n  renderPerRepeater(doc, stats.perRepeater);\n  renderExerciseTable(doc, exercises);\n\n  if (stats.blStats.length > 0) {\n    doc.addPage();\n    renderAustriaMap(doc, stats.blStats);\n  }\n\n  const chartData = stats.bezirkStats.length > 0\n    ? stats.bezirkStats.map(bz => ({ label: `${bz.bundesland} ${bz.bezirk_code}`, participants: bz.participants, reports: bz.reports }))\n    : stats.blStats.map(bl => ({ label: bl.bundesland, participants: bl.participants, reports: bl.reports }));\n\n  if (chartData.length > 0) {\n    renderBarChart(doc, chartData);\n    renderPieChart(doc, chartData.map(d => ({ label: d.label, value: d.reports })));\n  }\n\n  if (stats.bezirkStats.length > 0) {\n    doc.addPage();\n    renderBezirkTable(doc, stats.bezirkStats, stats);\n  } else if (stats.blStats.length > 0) {\n    doc.addPage();\n    renderBlTable(doc, stats.blStats, stats);\n  }\n\n  doc.addPage();\n  renderParticipants(doc, stats.participants);\n  renderFooter(doc);\n\n  doc.end();\n  return doc;\n}\n\nexport function generateExercisePdf(\n  exercise: { name: string; date: string; exercise_type?: string | null },\n  stats: PdfStats,\n): PDFKit.PDFDocument {\n  const doc = new PDFDocument({\n    layout: 'landscape',\n    size: 'A4',\n    margins: { top: 30, bottom: 30, left: 40, right: 40 },\n    autoFirstPage: true,\n    bufferPages: true,\n  });\n\n  const formattedDate = new Date(exercise.date + 'T00:00:00').toLocaleDateString('de-AT', {\n    weekday: 'long', day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric',\n  });\n  renderHeader(doc, 'Auswertung', `${exercise.name} — ${formattedDate}`);\n  renderSummaryCards(doc, stats);\n  renderPerRepeater(doc, stats.perRepeater);\n\n  if (stats.blStats.length > 0) {\n    doc.addPage();\n    renderAustriaMap(doc, stats.blStats);\n  }\n\n  const chartData = stats.bezirkStats.length > 0\n    ? stats.bezirkStats.map(bz => ({ label: `${bz.bundesland} ${bz.bezirk_code}`, participants: bz.participants, reports: bz.reports }))\n    : stats.blStats.map(bl => ({ label: bl.bundesland, participants: bl.participants, reports: bl.reports }));\n\n  if (chartData.length > 0) {\n    renderBarChart(doc, chartData);\n    renderPieChart(doc, chartData.map(d => ({ label: d.label, value: d.reports })));\n  }\n\n  if (stats.bezirkStats.length > 0) {\n    doc.addPage();\n    renderBezirkTable(doc, stats.bezirkStats, stats);\n  } else if (stats.blStats.length > 0) {\n    doc.addPage();\n    renderBlTable(doc, stats.blStats, stats);\n  }\n\n  doc.addPage();\n  renderParticipants(doc, stats.participants);\n  renderFooter(doc);\n\n  doc.end();\n  return doc;\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

Deployed successfully. The PDF fixes are now live:

1. **Austria map** — hex colors (`#rrggbb`) instead of CSS `rgb()`, `fillAndStroke()` instead of chained `fill().stroke()`
2. **Table layouts** — `textAt()` helper with `lineBreak: false` and manual Y tracking prevents column misalignment

You can test by downloading a PDF from the Auswertung page — the map should show proper heatmap colors and tables should be cleanly aligned.

    82→    d: 'M550.84,84.85L551.18,84.93L551.18,84.93L551.24,84.74L551.24,84.74L553.13,84.46L553.13,84.46L554.28,86.01L554.28,86.01L554.41,86.46L554.41,86.46L554.55,87.92L557.14,89.89L557.58,87.83L558.77,87.91L560.11,88.61L559.72,90.15L560.84,91.36L562.81,91.53L561.98,101.14L563.03,101.53L563.26,103.45L565.04,103.68L565.32,105.77L565.32,105.77L564.65,106.48L564.65,106.48L560.19,103.85L553.72,106.47L553.97,108.53L545.36,106.43L544.1,108.53L540.76,106.87L536.85,108.01L538.09,106.6L536.79,106.67L535.86,105.13L536.57,104.42L535,104.28L533.75,102.39L535.26,98.75L534.96,96.26L533.71,96.06L534.81,95.39L535.53,91.49L536.19,91.86L536.19,91.86L538.17,94.06L538.17,94.06L539.58,93.98L542.22,90.47L545.7,88.94L545.7,88.94L546.89,88.2L547.42,88.95L547.42,88.95L549.17,88.44L549.4,86.68L549.4,86.68L548.97,85.27L548.97,85.27L550.58,84.72L550.58,84.72z',
    83→    labelX: 550, labelY: 97,
    84→  },
    85→};
    86→
    87→const BL_SHORT: Record<string, string> = {
    88→  '01': 'OE1', '02': 'OE2', '03': 'OE3', '04': 'OE4', '05': 'OE5',
    89→  '06': 'OE6', '07': 'OE7', '08': 'OE8', '09': 'OE9',
    90→};
    91→
    92→// ── Helper functions ─────────────────────────────────────
    93→
    94→function getHeatColor(value: number, max: number): string {
    95→  if (max === 0 || value === 0) return '#e5e7eb';
    96→  const t = value / max;
    97→  const r = Math.round(194 + (30 - 194) * t);
    98→  const g = Math.round(213 + (58 - 213) * t);
    99→  const b = Math.round(236 + (95 - 236) * t);
   100→  return '#' + [r, g, b].map(c => c.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
   101→}
   102→
   103→function formatDateDE(d: string): string {
   104→  const date = new Date(d + 'T00:00:00');
   105→  return date.toLocaleDateString('de-AT', { day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric' });
   106→}
   107→
   108→function ensureSpace(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, needed: number) {
   109→  const bottom = doc.page.height - doc.page.margins.bottom;
   110→  if (doc.y + needed > bottom) {
   111→    doc.addPage();
   112→  }
   113→}
   114→
   115→/** Write text at fixed position without moving doc.y */
   116→function textAt(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, str: string, x: number, y: number, opts?: PDFKit.Mixins.TextOptions) {
   117→  doc.text(str, x, y, { lineBreak: false, ...opts });
   118→}
   119→
   120→// ── Render functions ─────────────────────────────────────
   121→
   122→function renderHeader(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, title: string, subtitle: string) {
   123→  const pageW = doc.page.width;
   124→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   125→  const barW = pageW - m.left - m.right;
   126→
   127→  doc.save();
   128→  doc.rect(m.left, m.top, barW, 50).fill(NAVY);
   129→  doc.fontSize(18).fillColor(WHITE);
   130→  textAt(doc, 'BOS-ARSA Log', m.left + 15, m.top + 10);
   131→  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor('#93c5fd');
   132→  textAt(doc, 'Im Sinne der Sicherheit', m.left + 15, m.top + 32);
   133→  doc.restore();
   134→
   135→  doc.y = m.top + 60;
   136→  doc.fontSize(14).fillColor(NAVY);
   137→  textAt(doc, title, m.left, doc.y);
   138→  doc.y += 18;
   139→  doc.fontSize(9).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   140→  textAt(doc, subtitle, m.left, doc.y);
   141→  doc.y += 16;
   142→}
   143→
   144→function renderSummaryCards(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, stats: PdfStats, exerciseCount?: number) {
   145→  ensureSpace(doc, 50);
   146→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   147→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   148→  const cols = exerciseCount !== undefined ? 4 : 2;
   149→  const cardW = usable / cols - 8;
   150→  const startY = doc.y;
   151→
   152→  const cards: { value: string | number; label: string }[] = [
   153→    { value: stats.totalParticipants, label: 'Teilnehmer' },
   154→    { value: stats.totalReports, label: 'Rapporte' },
   155→  ];
   156→  if (exerciseCount !== undefined) {
   157→    cards.push({ value: exerciseCount, label: 'Übungen' });
   158→    const avg = exerciseCount > 0 ? Math.round(stats.totalParticipants / exerciseCount * 10) / 10 : 0;
   159→    cards.push({ value: avg, label: 'Ø Teiln./Übung' });
   160→  }
   161→
   162→  cards.forEach((c, i) => {
   163→    const x = m.left + i * (cardW + 8);
   164→    doc.save();
   165→    doc.roundedRect(x, startY, cardW, 40, 4).fill(GRAY_50);
   166→    doc.fontSize(18).fillColor(NAVY);
   167→    textAt(doc, String(c.value), x, startY + 5, { width: cardW, align: 'center' });
   168→    doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   169→    textAt(doc, c.label, x, startY + 27, { width: cardW, align: 'center' });
   170→    doc.restore();
   171→  });
   172→  doc.y = startY + 48;
   173→}
   174→
   175→function renderPerRepeater(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, perRepeater: PerRepeater[]) {
   176→  if (perRepeater.length === 0) return;
   177→  ensureSpace(doc, 35);
   178→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   179→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   180→  const cols = Math.min(perRepeater.length, 6);
   181→  const cardW = usable / cols - 6;
   182→  const startY = doc.y;
   183→
   184→  perRepeater.slice(0, 6).forEach((r, i) => {
   185→    const x = m.left + i * (cardW + 6);
   186→    doc.save();
   187→    doc.roundedRect(x, startY, cardW, 28, 3).fill(GRAY_50);
   188→    doc.fontSize(12).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   189→    textAt(doc, String(r.count), x, startY + 2, { width: cardW, align: 'center' });
   190→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   191→    textAt(doc, r.short_name, x, startY + 17, { width: cardW, align: 'center' });
   192→    doc.restore();
   193→  });
   194→  doc.y = startY + 36;
   195→}
   196→
   197→function renderExerciseTable(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, exercises: ExerciseSummary[]) {
   198→  if (exercises.length === 0) return;
   199→  ensureSpace(doc, 60);
   200→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   201→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   202→
   203→  // Section header
   204→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 18).fill(NAVY);
   205→  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor(WHITE);
   206→  textAt(doc, `Übungen (${exercises.length})`, m.left + 8, doc.y + 4);
   207→  doc.y += 20;
   208→
   209→  // Column positions
   210→  const colDate = m.left + 5;
   211→  const colName = m.left + 80;
   212→  const colPart = m.left + usable - 90;
   213→  const colRap = m.left + usable - 40;
   214→
   215→  // Column headers
   216→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 14).fill(GRAY_100);
   217→  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   218→  const hy = doc.y + 4;
   219→  textAt(doc, 'DATUM', colDate, hy);
   220→  textAt(doc, 'NAME', colName, hy);
   221→  textAt(doc, 'TEILN.', colPart, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   222→  textAt(doc, 'RAPP.', colRap, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   223→  doc.y += 16;
   224→
   225→  exercises.forEach((ex, i) => {
   226→    ensureSpace(doc, 14);
   227→    const rowY = doc.y;
   228→    if (i % 2 === 0) {
   229→      doc.save();
   230→      doc.rect(m.left, rowY, usable, 12).fill(GRAY_50);
   231→      doc.restore();
   232→    }
   233→    doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   234→    textAt(doc, formatDateDE(ex.date), colDate, rowY + 2);
   235→    textAt(doc, ex.name || '', colName, rowY + 2, { width: colPart - colName - 10 });
   236→    textAt(doc, String(ex.participant_count), colPart, rowY + 2, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   237→    textAt(doc, String(ex.report_count), colRap, rowY + 2, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   238→    doc.y = rowY + 13;
   239→  });
   240→  doc.y += 4;
   241→}
   242→
   243→function renderAustriaMap(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, blStats: BlStat[]) {
   244→  if (blStats.length === 0) return;
   245→  ensureSpace(doc, 200);
   246→
   247→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   248→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   249→  const mapW = Math.min(usable, 400);
   250→  const mapH = mapW * 0.5;
   251→  const offsetX = m.left + (usable - mapW) / 2;
   252→
   253→  doc.fontSize(9).fillColor(NAVY);
   254→  textAt(doc, 'Teilnehmer nach Bundesland', m.left, doc.y);
   255→  doc.y += 14;
   256→  const mapStartY = doc.y;
   257→
   258→  const dataMap = new Map(blStats.map(bl => [bl.bundesland_code, bl]));
   259→  const maxP = Math.max(...blStats.map(bl => bl.participants), 1);
   260→
   261→  const svgW = 620;
   262→  const svgH = 300;
   263→  const scale = Math.min(mapW / svgW, mapH / svgH);
   264→
   265→  for (const [code, pathData] of Object.entries(BL_PATHS)) {
   266→    const bl = dataMap.get(code);
   267→    const participants = bl?.participants ?? 0;
   268→    const fillColor = getHeatColor(participants, maxP);
   269→
   270→    doc.save();
   271→    doc.translate(offsetX, mapStartY);
   272→    doc.scale(scale);
   273→    doc.path(pathData.d).lineWidth(2 / scale).fillAndStroke(fillColor, WHITE);
   274→    doc.restore();
   275→
   276→    // Label
   277→    const lx = offsetX + pathData.labelX * scale;
   278→    const ly = mapStartY + pathData.labelY * scale;
   279→    doc.save();
   280→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(NAVY);
   281→    textAt(doc, BL_SHORT[code] || code, lx - 15, ly - 6, { width: 30, align: 'center' });
   282→    doc.fontSize(5).fillColor(NAVY);
   283→    textAt(doc, String(participants), lx - 15, ly + 1, { width: 30, align: 'center' });
   284→    doc.restore();
   285→  }
   286→
   287→  doc.y = mapStartY + mapH + 10;
   288→}
   289→
   290→function renderBarChart(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, chartData: { label: string; participants: number; reports: number }[]) {
   291→  if (chartData.length === 0) return;
   292→  ensureSpace(doc, 180);
   293→
   294→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   295→  const chartW = Math.min(doc.page.width - m.left - m.right, 500);
   296→  const chartH = 140;
   297→  const startX = m.left;
   298→
   299→  doc.fontSize(9).fillColor(NAVY);
   300→  textAt(doc, 'Stationen & Rapporte', m.left, doc.y);
   301→  doc.y += 15;
   302→  const startY = doc.y;
   303→
   304→  const maxVal = Math.max(...chartData.map(d => Math.max(d.participants, d.reports)), 1);
   305→  const barAreaW = chartW - 30;
   306→  const groupW = barAreaW / chartData.length;
   307→  const barW = Math.min(groupW * 0.35, 20);
   308→
   309→  // Y-axis gridlines
   310→  for (let i = 0; i <= 4; i++) {
   311→    const y = startY + chartH - (chartH * i / 4);
   312→    const val = Math.round(maxVal * i / 4);
   313→    doc.save();
   314→    doc.strokeColor(GRAY_200).lineWidth(0.5).moveTo(startX + 25, y).lineTo(startX + chartW, y).stroke();
   315→    doc.fontSize(5).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   316→    textAt(doc, String(val), startX, y - 3, { width: 22, align: 'right' });
   317→    doc.restore();
   318→  }
   319→
   320→  // Bars
   321→  chartData.forEach((d, i) => {
   322→    const groupX = startX + 30 + i * groupW;
   323→    const pH = (d.participants / maxVal) * chartH;
   324→    doc.rect(groupX, startY + chartH - pH, barW, pH).fill(NAVY);
   325→    const rH = (d.reports / maxVal) * chartH;
   326→    doc.rect(groupX + barW + 2, startY + chartH - rH, barW, rH).fill(RED);
   327→    doc.save();
   328→    doc.fontSize(4).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   329→    textAt(doc, d.label, groupX - 2, startY + chartH + 3, { width: groupW });
   330→    doc.restore();
   331→  });
   332→
   333→  // Legend
   334→  const legendY = startY + chartH + 14;
   335→  doc.rect(startX + 30, legendY, 8, 6).fill(NAVY);
   336→  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   337→  textAt(doc, 'Stationen', startX + 42, legendY);
   338→  doc.rect(startX + 90, legendY, 8, 6).fill(RED);
   339→  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   340→  textAt(doc, 'Rapporte', startX + 102, legendY);
   341→  doc.y = legendY + 14;
   342→}
   343→
   344→function renderPieChart(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, chartData: { label: string; value: number }[]) {
   345→  const filtered = chartData.filter(d => d.value > 0);
   346→  if (filtered.length === 0) return;
   347→  ensureSpace(doc, 180);
   348→
   349→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   350→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   351→
   352→  doc.fontSize(9).fillColor(NAVY);
   353→  textAt(doc, 'Rapporte Verteilung', m.left, doc.y);
   354→  doc.y += 12;
   355→
   356→  const cx = m.left + 100;
   357→  const cy = doc.y + 70;
   358→  const r = 60;
   359→  const total = filtered.reduce((s, d) => s + d.value, 0);
   360→  let startAngle = -Math.PI / 2;
   361→
   362→  filtered.forEach((d, i) => {
   363→    const sliceAngle = (d.value / total) * 2 * Math.PI;
   364→    const endAngle = startAngle + sliceAngle;
   365→    const color = PIE_COLORS[i % PIE_COLORS.length];
   366→
   367→    const x1 = cx + r * Math.cos(startAngle);
   368→    const y1 = cy + r * Math.sin(startAngle);
   369→    const x2 = cx + r * Math.cos(endAngle);
   370→    const y2 = cy + r * Math.sin(endAngle);
   371→    const largeArc = sliceAngle > Math.PI ? 1 : 0;
   372→
   373→    doc.path(`M ${cx} ${cy} L ${x1} ${y1} A ${r} ${r} 0 ${largeArc} 1 ${x2} ${y2} Z`).fill(color);
   374→    startAngle = endAngle;
   375→  });
   376→
   377→  // Legend
   378→  const legendX = cx + r + 30;
   379→  filtered.forEach((d, i) => {
   380→    const ly = cy - r + i * 12;
   381→    if (ly > doc.page.height - m.bottom) return;
   382→    const color = PIE_COLORS[i % PIE_COLORS.length];
   383→    doc.rect(legendX, ly, 8, 8).fill(color);
   384→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   385→    textAt(doc, `${d.label} (${d.value})`, legendX + 12, ly + 1, { width: usable - legendX + m.left - 12 });
   386→  });
   387→
   388→  doc.y = cy + r + 15;
   389→}
   390→
   391→function renderBezirkTable(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, bezirkStats: BezirkStat[], stats: PdfStats) {
   392→  if (bezirkStats.length === 0) return;
   393→  ensureSpace(doc, 60);
   394→
   395→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   396→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   397→  const colName = m.left + 5;
   398→  const colPart = m.left + usable - 90;
   399→  const colRap = m.left + usable - 40;
   400→
   401→  // Section header
   402→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 18).fill(NAVY);
   403→  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor(WHITE);
   404→  textAt(doc, 'Nebenstationen nach Bezirk', m.left + 8, doc.y + 4);
   405→  doc.y += 20;
   406→
   407→  // Column headers
   408→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 14).fill(GRAY_100);
   409→  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   410→  const hy = doc.y + 4;
   411→  textAt(doc, 'BUNDESLAND / BEZIRK', colName, hy);
   412→  textAt(doc, 'TEILN.', colPart, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   413→  textAt(doc, 'RAPP.', colRap, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   414→  doc.y += 16;
   415→
   416→  // Group by Bundesland
   417→  const byBl: Record<string, { name: string; bezirke: BezirkStat[]; totalP: number; totalR: number }> = {};
   418→  for (const bz of bezirkStats) {
   419→    if (!byBl[bz.bundesland_code]) {
   420→      byBl[bz.bundesland_code] = { name: bz.bundesland, bezirke: [], totalP: 0, totalR: 0 };
   421→    }
   422→    byBl[bz.bundesland_code].bezirke.push(bz);
   423→    byBl[bz.bundesland_code].totalP += bz.participants;
   424→    byBl[bz.bundesland_code].totalR += bz.reports;
   425→  }
   426→
   427→  for (const [, bl] of Object.entries(byBl)) {
   428→    ensureSpace(doc, 14);
   429→    const rowY = doc.y;
   430→    doc.rect(m.left, rowY, usable, 13).fill(GRAY_100);
   431→    doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   432→    textAt(doc, bl.name, colName, rowY + 3);
   433→    textAt(doc, String(bl.totalP), colPart, rowY + 3, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   434→    textAt(doc, String(bl.totalR), colRap, rowY + 3, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   435→    doc.y = rowY + 14;
   436→
   437→    bl.bezirke.forEach(bz => {
   438→      ensureSpace(doc, 12);
   439→      const ry = doc.y;
   440→      doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_MED);
   441→      textAt(doc, `    ${bz.bezirk_code}  ${bz.bezirk_name}`, colName, ry + 1);
   442→      textAt(doc, String(bz.participants), colPart, ry + 1, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   443→      textAt(doc, String(bz.reports), colRap, ry + 1, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   444→      doc.y = ry + 11;
   445→    });
   446→  }
   447→
   448→  // Gesamt
   449→  ensureSpace(doc, 18);
   450→  const gy = doc.y;
   451→  doc.rect(m.left, gy, usable, 16).fill(NAVY);
   452→  doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(WHITE);
   453→  textAt(doc, 'Gesamt', colName, gy + 4);
   454→  textAt(doc, String(stats.totalParticipants), colPart, gy + 4, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   455→  textAt(doc, String(stats.totalReports), colRap, gy + 4, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   456→  doc.y = gy + 20;
   457→}
   458→
   459→function renderBlTable(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, blStats: BlStat[], stats: PdfStats) {
   460→  if (blStats.length === 0) return;
   461→  ensureSpace(doc, 60);
   462→
   463→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   464→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   465→  const colName = m.left + 5;
   466→  const colPart = m.left + usable - 90;
   467→  const colRap = m.left + usable - 40;
   468→
   469→  // Section header
   470→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 18).fill(NAVY);
   471→  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor(WHITE);
   472→  textAt(doc, 'Teilnehmer nach Bundesland / Land', m.left + 8, doc.y + 4);
   473→  doc.y += 20;
   474→
   475→  // Column headers
   476→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 14).fill(GRAY_100);
   477→  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   478→  const hy = doc.y + 4;
   479→  textAt(doc, 'BUNDESLAND', colName, hy);
   480→  textAt(doc, 'TEILNEHMER', colPart, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   481→  textAt(doc, 'RAPPORTE', colRap, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   482→  doc.y += 16;
   483→
   484→  blStats.forEach((bl, i) => {
   485→    ensureSpace(doc, 12);
   486→    const rowY = doc.y;
   487→    if (i % 2 === 0) {
   488→      doc.save();
   489→      doc.rect(m.left, rowY, usable, 12).fill(GRAY_50);
   490→      doc.restore();
   491→    }
   492→    doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   493→    textAt(doc, bl.bundesland, colName, rowY + 2);
   494→    textAt(doc, String(bl.participants), colPart, rowY + 2, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   495→    textAt(doc, String(bl.reports), colRap, rowY + 2, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   496→    doc.y = rowY + 13;
   497→  });
   498→
   499→  // Gesamt
   500→  ensureSpace(doc, 18);
   501→  const gy = doc.y;
   502→  doc.rect(m.left, gy, usable, 16).fill(NAVY);
   503→  doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(WHITE);
   504→  textAt(doc, 'Gesamt', colName, gy + 4);
   505→  textAt(doc, String(stats.totalParticipants), colPart, gy + 4, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   506→  textAt(doc, String(stats.totalReports), colRap, gy + 4, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   507→  doc.y = gy + 20;
   508→}
   509→
   510→function renderParticipants(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, participants: Participant[]) {
   511→  if (participants.length === 0) return;
   512→  ensureSpace(doc, 60);
   513→
   514→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   515→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   516→
   517→  // Section header
   518→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 18).fill(NAVY);
   519→  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor(WHITE);
   520→  textAt(doc, `Teilnehmer-Liste (${participants.length})`, m.left + 8, doc.y + 4);
   521→  doc.y += 20;
   522→
   523→  // Column positions
   524→  const colNum = m.left + 3;
   525→  const colCall = m.left + 25;
   526→  const colName = m.left + 90;
   527→  const colBz = m.left + usable - 135;
   528→  const colBl = m.left + usable - 90;
   529→  const colRap = m.left + usable - 35;
   530→
   531→  // Column headers
   532→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 14).fill(GRAY_100);
   533→  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   534→  const hy = doc.y + 4;
   535→  textAt(doc, '#', colNum, hy);
   536→  textAt(doc, 'RUFZEICHEN', colCall, hy);
   537→  textAt(doc, 'NAME', colName, hy);
   538→  textAt(doc, 'BEZ.', colBz, hy);
   539→  textAt(doc, 'BUNDESLAND', colBl, hy);
   540→  textAt(doc, 'RAPP.', colRap, hy, { width: 35, align: 'right' });
   541→  doc.y += 16;
   542→
   543→  participants.forEach((p, i) => {
   544→    ensureSpace(doc, 11);
   545→    const rowY = doc.y;
   546→    if (i % 2 === 0) {
   547→      doc.save();
   548→      doc.rect(m.left, rowY, usable, 10).fill(GRAY_50);
   549→      doc.restore();
   550→    }
   551→    const ty = rowY + 2;
   552→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   553→    textAt(doc, String(i + 1), colNum, ty);
   554→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   555→    textAt(doc, p.callsign, colCall, ty);
   556→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_MED);
   557→    textAt(doc, p.name || '', colName, ty, { width: colBz - colName - 5 });
   558→    textAt(doc, p.bezirk_code || '', colBz, ty);
   559→    textAt(doc, p.bundesland_name || '', colBl, ty, { width: colRap - colBl - 5 });
   560→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   561→    textAt(doc, String(p.report_count), colRap, ty, { width: 35, align: 'right' });
   562→    doc.y = rowY + 11;
   563→  });
   564→  doc.y += 4;
   565→}
   566→
   567→function renderFooter(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument) {
   568→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   569→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   570→  ensureSpace(doc, 25);
   571→  doc.strokeColor(GRAY_200).lineWidth(0.5).moveTo(m.left, doc.y).lineTo(m.left + usable, doc.y).stroke();
   572→  doc.y += 8;
   573→  doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   574→  textAt(doc, 'oeradio.at  ·  bosarsa.oeradio.at', m.left, doc.y, { width: usable, align: 'center' });
   575→}
   576→
   577→// ── Public API ────────────────────────────────────────────
   578→
   579→export function generateAggregatedPdf(
   580→  exercises: ExerciseSummary[],
   581→  stats: PdfStats,
   582→  from: string,
   583→  to: string,
   584→): PDFKit.PDFDocument {
   585→  const doc = new PDFDocument({
   586→    layout: 'landscape',
   587→    size: 'A4',
   588→    margins: { top: 30, bottom: 30, left: 40, right: 40 },
   589→    autoFirstPage: true,
   590→    bufferPages: true,
   591→  });
   592→
   593→  renderHeader(doc, 'Auswertung', `${formatDateDE(from)} – ${formatDateDE(to)}  ·  ${exercises.length} Übung${exercises.length !== 1 ? 'en' : ''}`);
   594→  renderSummaryCards(doc, stats, exercises.length);
   595→  renderPerRepeater(doc, stats.perRepeater);
   596→  renderExerciseTable(doc, exercises);
   597→
   598→  if (stats.blStats.length > 0) {
   599→    doc.addPage();
   600→    renderAustriaMap(doc, stats.blStats);
   601→  }
   602→
   603→  const chartData = stats.bezirkStats.length > 0
   604→    ? stats.bezirkStats.map(bz => ({ label: `${bz.bundesland} ${bz.bezirk_code}`, participants: bz.participants, reports: bz.reports }))
   605→    : stats.blStats.map(bl => ({ label: bl.bundesland, participants: bl.participants, reports: bl.reports }));
   606→
   607→  if (chartData.length > 0) {
   608→    renderBarChart(doc, chartData);
   609→    renderPieChart(doc, chartData.map(d => ({ label: d.label, value: d.reports })));
   610→  }
   611→
   612→  if (stats.bezirkStats.length > 0) {
   613→    doc.addPage();
   614→    renderBezirkTable(doc, stats.bezirkStats, stats);
   615→  } else if (stats.blStats.length > 0) {
   616→    doc.addPage();
   617→    renderBlTable(doc, stats.blStats, stats);
   618→  }
   619→
   620→  doc.addPage();
   621→  renderParticipants(doc, stats.participants);
   622→  renderFooter(doc);
   623→
   624→  doc.end();
   625→  return doc;
   626→}
   627→
   628→export function generateExercisePdf(
   629→  exercise: { name: string; date: string; exercise_type?: string | null },
   630→  stats: PdfStats,
   631→): PDFKit.PDFDocument {
   632→  const doc = new PDFDocument({
   633→    layout: 'landscape',
   634→    size: 'A4',
   635→    margins: { top: 30, bottom: 30, left: 40, right: 40 },
   636→    autoFirstPage: true,
   637→    bufferPages: true,
   638→  });
   639→
   640→  const formattedDate = new Date(exercise.date + 'T00:00:00').toLocaleDateString('de-AT', {
   641→    weekday: 'long', day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric',
   642→  });
   643→  renderHeader(doc, 'Auswertung', `${exercise.name} — ${formattedDate}`);
   644→  renderSummaryCards(doc, stats);
   645→  renderPerRepeater(doc, stats.perRepeater);
   646→
   647→  if (stats.blStats.length > 0) {
   648→    doc.addPage();
   649→    renderAustriaMap(doc, stats.blStats);
   650→  }
   651→
   652→  const chartData = stats.bezirkStats.length > 0
   653→    ? stats.bezirkStats.map(bz => ({ label: `${bz.bundesland} ${bz.bezirk_code}`, participants: bz.participants, reports: bz.reports }))
   654→    : stats.blStats.map(bl => ({ label: bl.bundesland, participants: bl.participants, reports: bl.reports }));
   655→
   656→  if (chartData.length > 0) {
   657→    renderBarChart(doc, chartData);
   658→    renderPieChart(doc, chartData.map(d => ({ label: d.label, value: d.reports })));
   659→  }
   660→
   661→  if (stats.bezirkStats.length > 0) {
   662→    doc.addPage();
   663→    renderBezirkTable(doc, stats.bezirkStats, stats);
   664→  } else if (stats.blStats.length > 0) {
   665→    doc.addPage();
   666→    renderBlTable(doc, stats.blStats, stats);
   667→  }
   668→
   669→  doc.addPage();
   670→  renderParticipants(doc, stats.participants);
   671→  renderFooter(doc);
   672→
   673→  doc.end();
   674→  return doc;
   675→}
   676→

> AGENT

The issue is clear — the map labels are always `NAVY` (#1e3a5f), but when a Bundesland has high participation (like Kärnten), the heatmap background is also dark, making the text invisible. I need to compute text color based on background brightness.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_012s3zfzqdQVRYUtACJMnxY9
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/services/pdf.ts",
  "old_string": "function getHeatColor(value: number, max: number): string {\n  if (max === 0 || value === 0) return '#e5e7eb';\n  const t = value / max;\n  const r = Math.round(194 + (30 - 194) * t);\n  const g = Math.round(213 + (58 - 213) * t);\n  const b = Math.round(236 + (95 - 236) * t);\n  return '#' + [r, g, b].map(c => c.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');\n}",
  "new_string": "function getHeatColor(value: number, max: number): string {\n  if (max === 0 || value === 0) return '#e5e7eb';\n  const t = value / max;\n  const r = Math.round(194 + (30 - 194) * t);\n  const g = Math.round(213 + (58 - 213) * t);\n  const b = Math.round(236 + (95 - 236) * t);\n  return '#' + [r, g, b].map(c => c.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');\n}\n\n/** Return white for dark backgrounds, navy for light backgrounds */\nfunction getLabelColor(hexBg: string): string {\n  const r = parseInt(hexBg.slice(1, 3), 16);\n  const g = parseInt(hexBg.slice(3, 5), 16);\n  const b = parseInt(hexBg.slice(5, 7), 16);\n  // Relative luminance (perceived brightness)\n  const lum = (0.299 * r + 0.587 * g + 0.114 * b);\n  return lum < 150 ? WHITE : NAVY;\n}"
}

Deployed. Die Kartentext-Labels passen sich jetzt dem Hintergrund an:

- **Heller Hintergrund** (wenig/keine Teilnehmer) → dunkler Navy-Text
- **Dunkler Hintergrund** (viele Teilnehmer, z.B. Kärnten) → weißer Text

Die `getLabelColor()` Funktion berechnet die wahrgenommene Helligkeit des Heatmap-Hintergrunds und wechselt bei Luminanz < 150 auf Weiß.

    82→    d: 'M550.84,84.85L551.18,84.93L551.18,84.93L551.24,84.74L551.24,84.74L553.13,84.46L553.13,84.46L554.28,86.01L554.28,86.01L554.41,86.46L554.41,86.46L554.55,87.92L557.14,89.89L557.58,87.83L558.77,87.91L560.11,88.61L559.72,90.15L560.84,91.36L562.81,91.53L561.98,101.14L563.03,101.53L563.26,103.45L565.04,103.68L565.32,105.77L565.32,105.77L564.65,106.48L564.65,106.48L560.19,103.85L553.72,106.47L553.97,108.53L545.36,106.43L544.1,108.53L540.76,106.87L536.85,108.01L538.09,106.6L536.79,106.67L535.86,105.13L536.57,104.42L535,104.28L533.75,102.39L535.26,98.75L534.96,96.26L533.71,96.06L534.81,95.39L535.53,91.49L536.19,91.86L536.19,91.86L538.17,94.06L538.17,94.06L539.58,93.98L542.22,90.47L545.7,88.94L545.7,88.94L546.89,88.2L547.42,88.95L547.42,88.95L549.17,88.44L549.4,86.68L549.4,86.68L548.97,85.27L548.97,85.27L550.58,84.72L550.58,84.72z',
    83→    labelX: 550, labelY: 97,
    84→  },
    85→};
    86→
    87→const BL_SHORT: Record<string, string> = {
    88→  '01': 'OE1', '02': 'OE2', '03': 'OE3', '04': 'OE4', '05': 'OE5',
    89→  '06': 'OE6', '07': 'OE7', '08': 'OE8', '09': 'OE9',
    90→};
    91→
    92→// ── Helper functions ─────────────────────────────────────
    93→
    94→function getHeatColor(value: number, max: number): string {
    95→  if (max === 0 || value === 0) return '#e5e7eb';
    96→  const t = value / max;
    97→  const r = Math.round(194 + (30 - 194) * t);
    98→  const g = Math.round(213 + (58 - 213) * t);
    99→  const b = Math.round(236 + (95 - 236) * t);
   100→  return '#' + [r, g, b].map(c => c.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
   101→}
   102→
   103→/** Return white for dark backgrounds, navy for light backgrounds */
   104→function getLabelColor(hexBg: string): string {
   105→  const r = parseInt(hexBg.slice(1, 3), 16);
   106→  const g = parseInt(hexBg.slice(3, 5), 16);
   107→  const b = parseInt(hexBg.slice(5, 7), 16);
   108→  // Relative luminance (perceived brightness)
   109→  const lum = (0.299 * r + 0.587 * g + 0.114 * b);
   110→  return lum < 150 ? WHITE : NAVY;
   111→}
   112→
   113→function formatDateDE(d: string): string {
   114→  const date = new Date(d + 'T00:00:00');
   115→  return date.toLocaleDateString('de-AT', { day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric' });
   116→}
   117→
   118→function ensureSpace(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, needed: number) {
   119→  const bottom = doc.page.height - doc.page.margins.bottom;
   120→  if (doc.y + needed > bottom) {
   121→    doc.addPage();
   122→  }
   123→}
   124→
   125→/** Write text at fixed position without moving doc.y */
   126→function textAt(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, str: string, x: number, y: number, opts?: PDFKit.Mixins.TextOptions) {
   127→  doc.text(str, x, y, { lineBreak: false, ...opts });
   128→}
   129→
   130→// ── Render functions ─────────────────────────────────────
   131→
   132→function renderHeader(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, title: string, subtitle: string) {
   133→  const pageW = doc.page.width;
   134→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   135→  const barW = pageW - m.left - m.right;
   136→
   137→  doc.save();
   138→  doc.rect(m.left, m.top, barW, 50).fill(NAVY);
   139→  doc.fontSize(18).fillColor(WHITE);
   140→  textAt(doc, 'BOS-ARSA Log', m.left + 15, m.top + 10);
   141→  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor('#93c5fd');
   142→  textAt(doc, 'Im Sinne der Sicherheit', m.left + 15, m.top + 32);
   143→  doc.restore();
   144→
   145→  doc.y = m.top + 60;
   146→  doc.fontSize(14).fillColor(NAVY);
   147→  textAt(doc, title, m.left, doc.y);
   148→  doc.y += 18;
   149→  doc.fontSize(9).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   150→  textAt(doc, subtitle, m.left, doc.y);
   151→  doc.y += 16;
   152→}
   153→
   154→function renderSummaryCards(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, stats: PdfStats, exerciseCount?: number) {
   155→  ensureSpace(doc, 50);
   156→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   157→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   158→  const cols = exerciseCount !== undefined ? 4 : 2;
   159→  const cardW = usable / cols - 8;
   160→  const startY = doc.y;
   161→
   162→  const cards: { value: string | number; label: string }[] = [
   163→    { value: stats.totalParticipants, label: 'Teilnehmer' },
   164→    { value: stats.totalReports, label: 'Rapporte' },
   165→  ];
   166→  if (exerciseCount !== undefined) {
   167→    cards.push({ value: exerciseCount, label: 'Übungen' });
   168→    const avg = exerciseCount > 0 ? Math.round(stats.totalParticipants / exerciseCount * 10) / 10 : 0;
   169→    cards.push({ value: avg, label: 'Ø Teiln./Übung' });
   170→  }
   171→
   172→  cards.forEach((c, i) => {
   173→    const x = m.left + i * (cardW + 8);
   174→    doc.save();
   175→    doc.roundedRect(x, startY, cardW, 40, 4).fill(GRAY_50);
   176→    doc.fontSize(18).fillColor(NAVY);
   177→    textAt(doc, String(c.value), x, startY + 5, { width: cardW, align: 'center' });
   178→    doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   179→    textAt(doc, c.label, x, startY + 27, { width: cardW, align: 'center' });
   180→    doc.restore();
   181→  });
   182→  doc.y = startY + 48;
   183→}
   184→
   185→function renderPerRepeater(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, perRepeater: PerRepeater[]) {
   186→  if (perRepeater.length === 0) return;
   187→  ensureSpace(doc, 35);
   188→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   189→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   190→  const cols = Math.min(perRepeater.length, 6);
   191→  const cardW = usable / cols - 6;
   192→  const startY = doc.y;
   193→
   194→  perRepeater.slice(0, 6).forEach((r, i) => {
   195→    const x = m.left + i * (cardW + 6);
   196→    doc.save();
   197→    doc.roundedRect(x, startY, cardW, 28, 3).fill(GRAY_50);
   198→    doc.fontSize(12).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   199→    textAt(doc, String(r.count), x, startY + 2, { width: cardW, align: 'center' });
   200→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   201→    textAt(doc, r.short_name, x, startY + 17, { width: cardW, align: 'center' });
   202→    doc.restore();
   203→  });
   204→  doc.y = startY + 36;
   205→}
   206→
   207→function renderExerciseTable(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, exercises: ExerciseSummary[]) {
   208→  if (exercises.length === 0) return;
   209→  ensureSpace(doc, 60);
   210→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   211→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   212→
   213→  // Section header
   214→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 18).fill(NAVY);
   215→  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor(WHITE);
   216→  textAt(doc, `Übungen (${exercises.length})`, m.left + 8, doc.y + 4);
   217→  doc.y += 20;
   218→
   219→  // Column positions
   220→  const colDate = m.left + 5;
   221→  const colName = m.left + 80;
   222→  const colPart = m.left + usable - 90;
   223→  const colRap = m.left + usable - 40;
   224→
   225→  // Column headers
   226→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 14).fill(GRAY_100);
   227→  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   228→  const hy = doc.y + 4;
   229→  textAt(doc, 'DATUM', colDate, hy);
   230→  textAt(doc, 'NAME', colName, hy);
   231→  textAt(doc, 'TEILN.', colPart, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   232→  textAt(doc, 'RAPP.', colRap, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   233→  doc.y += 16;
   234→
   235→  exercises.forEach((ex, i) => {
   236→    ensureSpace(doc, 14);
   237→    const rowY = doc.y;
   238→    if (i % 2 === 0) {
   239→      doc.save();
   240→      doc.rect(m.left, rowY, usable, 12).fill(GRAY_50);
   241→      doc.restore();
   242→    }
   243→    doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   244→    textAt(doc, formatDateDE(ex.date), colDate, rowY + 2);
   245→    textAt(doc, ex.name || '', colName, rowY + 2, { width: colPart - colName - 10 });
   246→    textAt(doc, String(ex.participant_count), colPart, rowY + 2, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   247→    textAt(doc, String(ex.report_count), colRap, rowY + 2, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   248→    doc.y = rowY + 13;
   249→  });
   250→  doc.y += 4;
   251→}
   252→
   253→function renderAustriaMap(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, blStats: BlStat[]) {
   254→  if (blStats.length === 0) return;
   255→  ensureSpace(doc, 200);
   256→
   257→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   258→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   259→  const mapW = Math.min(usable, 400);
   260→  const mapH = mapW * 0.5;
   261→  const offsetX = m.left + (usable - mapW) / 2;
   262→
   263→  doc.fontSize(9).fillColor(NAVY);
   264→  textAt(doc, 'Teilnehmer nach Bundesland', m.left, doc.y);
   265→  doc.y += 14;
   266→  const mapStartY = doc.y;
   267→
   268→  const dataMap = new Map(blStats.map(bl => [bl.bundesland_code, bl]));
   269→  const maxP = Math.max(...blStats.map(bl => bl.participants), 1);
   270→
   271→  const svgW = 620;
   272→  const svgH = 300;
   273→  const scale = Math.min(mapW / svgW, mapH / svgH);
   274→
   275→  for (const [code, pathData] of Object.entries(BL_PATHS)) {
   276→    const bl = dataMap.get(code);
   277→    const participants = bl?.participants ?? 0;
   278→    const fillColor = getHeatColor(participants, maxP);
   279→
   280→    doc.save();
   281→    doc.translate(offsetX, mapStartY);
   282→    doc.scale(scale);
   283→    doc.path(pathData.d).lineWidth(2 / scale).fillAndStroke(fillColor, WHITE);
   284→    doc.restore();
   285→
   286→    // Label — contrast-aware color based on heatmap background
   287→    const lx = offsetX + pathData.labelX * scale;
   288→    const ly = mapStartY + pathData.labelY * scale;
   289→    const labelColor = getLabelColor(fillColor);
   290→    doc.save();
   291→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(labelColor);
   292→    textAt(doc, BL_SHORT[code] || code, lx - 15, ly - 6, { width: 30, align: 'center' });
   293→    doc.fontSize(5).fillColor(labelColor);
   294→    textAt(doc, String(participants), lx - 15, ly + 1, { width: 30, align: 'center' });
   295→    doc.restore();
   296→  }
   297→
   298→  doc.y = mapStartY + mapH + 10;
   299→}
   300→
   301→function renderBarChart(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, chartData: { label: string; participants: number; reports: number }[]) {
   302→  if (chartData.length === 0) return;
   303→  ensureSpace(doc, 180);
   304→
   305→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   306→  const chartW = Math.min(doc.page.width - m.left - m.right, 500);
   307→  const chartH = 140;
   308→  const startX = m.left;
   309→
   310→  doc.fontSize(9).fillColor(NAVY);
   311→  textAt(doc, 'Stationen & Rapporte', m.left, doc.y);
   312→  doc.y += 15;
   313→  const startY = doc.y;
   314→
   315→  const maxVal = Math.max(...chartData.map(d => Math.max(d.participants, d.reports)), 1);
   316→  const barAreaW = chartW - 30;
   317→  const groupW = barAreaW / chartData.length;
   318→  const barW = Math.min(groupW * 0.35, 20);
   319→
   320→  // Y-axis gridlines
   321→  for (let i = 0; i <= 4; i++) {
   322→    const y = startY + chartH - (chartH * i / 4);
   323→    const val = Math.round(maxVal * i / 4);
   324→    doc.save();
   325→    doc.strokeColor(GRAY_200).lineWidth(0.5).moveTo(startX + 25, y).lineTo(startX + chartW, y).stroke();
   326→    doc.fontSize(5).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   327→    textAt(doc, String(val), startX, y - 3, { width: 22, align: 'right' });
   328→    doc.restore();
   329→  }
   330→
   331→  // Bars
   332→  chartData.forEach((d, i) => {
   333→    const groupX = startX + 30 + i * groupW;
   334→    const pH = (d.participants / maxVal) * chartH;
   335→    doc.rect(groupX, startY + chartH - pH, barW, pH).fill(NAVY);
   336→    const rH = (d.reports / maxVal) * chartH;
   337→    doc.rect(groupX + barW + 2, startY + chartH - rH, barW, rH).fill(RED);
   338→    doc.save();
   339→    doc.fontSize(4).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   340→    textAt(doc, d.label, groupX - 2, startY + chartH + 3, { width: groupW });
   341→    doc.restore();
   342→  });
   343→
   344→  // Legend
   345→  const legendY = startY + chartH + 14;
   346→  doc.rect(startX + 30, legendY, 8, 6).fill(NAVY);
   347→  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   348→  textAt(doc, 'Stationen', startX + 42, legendY);
   349→  doc.rect(startX + 90, legendY, 8, 6).fill(RED);
   350→  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   351→  textAt(doc, 'Rapporte', startX + 102, legendY);
   352→  doc.y = legendY + 14;
   353→}
   354→
   355→function renderPieChart(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, chartData: { label: string; value: number }[]) {
   356→  const filtered = chartData.filter(d => d.value > 0);
   357→  if (filtered.length === 0) return;
   358→  ensureSpace(doc, 180);
   359→
   360→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   361→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   362→
   363→  doc.fontSize(9).fillColor(NAVY);
   364→  textAt(doc, 'Rapporte Verteilung', m.left, doc.y);
   365→  doc.y += 12;
   366→
   367→  const cx = m.left + 100;
   368→  const cy = doc.y + 70;
   369→  const r = 60;
   370→  const total = filtered.reduce((s, d) => s + d.value, 0);
   371→  let startAngle = -Math.PI / 2;
   372→
   373→  filtered.forEach((d, i) => {
   374→    const sliceAngle = (d.value / total) * 2 * Math.PI;
   375→    const endAngle = startAngle + sliceAngle;
   376→    const color = PIE_COLORS[i % PIE_COLORS.length];
   377→
   378→    const x1 = cx + r * Math.cos(startAngle);
   379→    const y1 = cy + r * Math.sin(startAngle);
   380→    const x2 = cx + r * Math.cos(endAngle);
   381→    const y2 = cy + r * Math.sin(endAngle);
   382→    const largeArc = sliceAngle > Math.PI ? 1 : 0;
   383→
   384→    doc.path(`M ${cx} ${cy} L ${x1} ${y1} A ${r} ${r} 0 ${largeArc} 1 ${x2} ${y2} Z`).fill(color);
   385→    startAngle = endAngle;
   386→  });
   387→
   388→  // Legend
   389→  const legendX = cx + r + 30;
   390→  filtered.forEach((d, i) => {
   391→    const ly = cy - r + i * 12;
   392→    if (ly > doc.page.height - m.bottom) return;
   393→    const color = PIE_COLORS[i % PIE_COLORS.length];
   394→    doc.rect(legendX, ly, 8, 8).fill(color);
   395→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   396→    textAt(doc, `${d.label} (${d.value})`, legendX + 12, ly + 1, { width: usable - legendX + m.left - 12 });
   397→  });
   398→
   399→  doc.y = cy + r + 15;
   400→}
   401→
   402→function renderBezirkTable(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, bezirkStats: BezirkStat[], stats: PdfStats) {
   403→  if (bezirkStats.length === 0) return;
   404→  ensureSpace(doc, 60);
   405→
   406→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   407→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   408→  const colName = m.left + 5;
   409→  const colPart = m.left + usable - 90;
   410→  const colRap = m.left + usable - 40;
   411→
   412→  // Section header
   413→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 18).fill(NAVY);
   414→  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor(WHITE);
   415→  textAt(doc, 'Nebenstationen nach Bezirk', m.left + 8, doc.y + 4);
   416→  doc.y += 20;
   417→
   418→  // Column headers
   419→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 14).fill(GRAY_100);
   420→  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   421→  const hy = doc.y + 4;
   422→  textAt(doc, 'BUNDESLAND / BEZIRK', colName, hy);
   423→  textAt(doc, 'TEILN.', colPart, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   424→  textAt(doc, 'RAPP.', colRap, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   425→  doc.y += 16;
   426→
   427→  // Group by Bundesland
   428→  const byBl: Record<string, { name: string; bezirke: BezirkStat[]; totalP: number; totalR: number }> = {};
   429→  for (const bz of bezirkStats) {
   430→    if (!byBl[bz.bundesland_code]) {
   431→      byBl[bz.bundesland_code] = { name: bz.bundesland, bezirke: [], totalP: 0, totalR: 0 };
   432→    }
   433→    byBl[bz.bundesland_code].bezirke.push(bz);
   434→    byBl[bz.bundesland_code].totalP += bz.participants;
   435→    byBl[bz.bundesland_code].totalR += bz.reports;
   436→  }
   437→
   438→  for (const [, bl] of Object.entries(byBl)) {
   439→    ensureSpace(doc, 14);
   440→    const rowY = doc.y;
   441→    doc.rect(m.left, rowY, usable, 13).fill(GRAY_100);
   442→    doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   443→    textAt(doc, bl.name, colName, rowY + 3);
   444→    textAt(doc, String(bl.totalP), colPart, rowY + 3, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   445→    textAt(doc, String(bl.totalR), colRap, rowY + 3, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   446→    doc.y = rowY + 14;
   447→
   448→    bl.bezirke.forEach(bz => {
   449→      ensureSpace(doc, 12);
   450→      const ry = doc.y;
   451→      doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_MED);
   452→      textAt(doc, `    ${bz.bezirk_code}  ${bz.bezirk_name}`, colName, ry + 1);
   453→      textAt(doc, String(bz.participants), colPart, ry + 1, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   454→      textAt(doc, String(bz.reports), colRap, ry + 1, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   455→      doc.y = ry + 11;
   456→    });
   457→  }
   458→
   459→  // Gesamt
   460→  ensureSpace(doc, 18);
   461→  const gy = doc.y;
   462→  doc.rect(m.left, gy, usable, 16).fill(NAVY);
   463→  doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(WHITE);
   464→  textAt(doc, 'Gesamt', colName, gy + 4);
   465→  textAt(doc, String(stats.totalParticipants), colPart, gy + 4, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   466→  textAt(doc, String(stats.totalReports), colRap, gy + 4, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   467→  doc.y = gy + 20;
   468→}
   469→
   470→function renderBlTable(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, blStats: BlStat[], stats: PdfStats) {
   471→  if (blStats.length === 0) return;
   472→  ensureSpace(doc, 60);
   473→
   474→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   475→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   476→  const colName = m.left + 5;
   477→  const colPart = m.left + usable - 90;
   478→  const colRap = m.left + usable - 40;
   479→
   480→  // Section header
   481→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 18).fill(NAVY);
   482→  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor(WHITE);
   483→  textAt(doc, 'Teilnehmer nach Bundesland / Land', m.left + 8, doc.y + 4);
   484→  doc.y += 20;
   485→
   486→  // Column headers
   487→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 14).fill(GRAY_100);
   488→  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   489→  const hy = doc.y + 4;
   490→  textAt(doc, 'BUNDESLAND', colName, hy);
   491→  textAt(doc, 'TEILNEHMER', colPart, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   492→  textAt(doc, 'RAPPORTE', colRap, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   493→  doc.y += 16;
   494→
   495→  blStats.forEach((bl, i) => {
   496→    ensureSpace(doc, 12);
   497→    const rowY = doc.y;
   498→    if (i % 2 === 0) {
   499→      doc.save();
   500→      doc.rect(m.left, rowY, usable, 12).fill(GRAY_50);
   501→      doc.restore();
   502→    }
   503→    doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   504→    textAt(doc, bl.bundesland, colName, rowY + 2);
   505→    textAt(doc, String(bl.participants), colPart, rowY + 2, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   506→    textAt(doc, String(bl.reports), colRap, rowY + 2, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   507→    doc.y = rowY + 13;
   508→  });
   509→
   510→  // Gesamt
   511→  ensureSpace(doc, 18);
   512→  const gy = doc.y;
   513→  doc.rect(m.left, gy, usable, 16).fill(NAVY);
   514→  doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(WHITE);
   515→  textAt(doc, 'Gesamt', colName, gy + 4);
   516→  textAt(doc, String(stats.totalParticipants), colPart, gy + 4, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   517→  textAt(doc, String(stats.totalReports), colRap, gy + 4, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   518→  doc.y = gy + 20;
   519→}
   520→
   521→function renderParticipants(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, participants: Participant[]) {
   522→  if (participants.length === 0) return;
   523→  ensureSpace(doc, 60);
   524→
   525→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   526→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   527→
   528→  // Section header
   529→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 18).fill(NAVY);
   530→  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor(WHITE);
   531→  textAt(doc, `Teilnehmer-Liste (${participants.length})`, m.left + 8, doc.y + 4);
   532→  doc.y += 20;
   533→
   534→  // Column positions
   535→  const colNum = m.left + 3;
   536→  const colCall = m.left + 25;
   537→  const colName = m.left + 90;
   538→  const colBz = m.left + usable - 135;
   539→  const colBl = m.left + usable - 90;
   540→  const colRap = m.left + usable - 35;
   541→
   542→  // Column headers
   543→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 14).fill(GRAY_100);
   544→  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   545→  const hy = doc.y + 4;
   546→  textAt(doc, '#', colNum, hy);
   547→  textAt(doc, 'RUFZEICHEN', colCall, hy);
   548→  textAt(doc, 'NAME', colName, hy);
   549→  textAt(doc, 'BEZ.', colBz, hy);
   550→  textAt(doc, 'BUNDESLAND', colBl, hy);
   551→  textAt(doc, 'RAPP.', colRap, hy, { width: 35, align: 'right' });
   552→  doc.y += 16;
   553→
   554→  participants.forEach((p, i) => {
   555→    ensureSpace(doc, 11);
   556→    const rowY = doc.y;
   557→    if (i % 2 === 0) {
   558→      doc.save();
   559→      doc.rect(m.left, rowY, usable, 10).fill(GRAY_50);
   560→      doc.restore();
   561→    }
   562→    const ty = rowY + 2;
   563→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   564→    textAt(doc, String(i + 1), colNum, ty);
   565→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   566→    textAt(doc, p.callsign, colCall, ty);
   567→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_MED);
   568→    textAt(doc, p.name || '', colName, ty, { width: colBz - colName - 5 });
   569→    textAt(doc, p.bezirk_code || '', colBz, ty);
   570→    textAt(doc, p.bundesland_name || '', colBl, ty, { width: colRap - colBl - 5 });
   571→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   572→    textAt(doc, String(p.report_count), colRap, ty, { width: 35, align: 'right' });
   573→    doc.y = rowY + 11;
   574→  });
   575→  doc.y += 4;
   576→}
   577→
   578→function renderFooter(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument) {
   579→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   580→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   581→  ensureSpace(doc, 25);
   582→  doc.strokeColor(GRAY_200).lineWidth(0.5).moveTo(m.left, doc.y).lineTo(m.left + usable, doc.y).stroke();
   583→  doc.y += 8;
   584→  doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   585→  textAt(doc, 'oeradio.at  ·  bosarsa.oeradio.at', m.left, doc.y, { width: usable, align: 'center' });
   586→}
   587→
   588→// ── Public API ────────────────────────────────────────────
   589→
   590→export function generateAggregatedPdf(
   591→  exercises: ExerciseSummary[],
   592→  stats: PdfStats,
   593→  from: string,
   594→  to: string,
   595→): PDFKit.PDFDocument {
   596→  const doc = new PDFDocument({
   597→    layout: 'landscape',
   598→    size: 'A4',
   599→    margins: { top: 30, bottom: 30, left: 40, right: 40 },
   600→    autoFirstPage: true,
   601→    bufferPages: true,
   602→  });
   603→
   604→  renderHeader(doc, 'Auswertung', `${formatDateDE(from)} – ${formatDateDE(to)}  ·  ${exercises.length} Übung${exercises.length !== 1 ? 'en' : ''}`);
   605→  renderSummaryCards(doc, stats, exercises.length);
   606→  renderPerRepeater(doc, stats.perRepeater);
   607→  renderExerciseTable(doc, exercises);
   608→
   609→  if (stats.blStats.length > 0) {
   610→    doc.addPage();
   611→    renderAustriaMap(doc, stats.blStats);
   612→  }
   613→
   614→  const chartData = stats.bezirkStats.length > 0
   615→    ? stats.bezirkStats.map(bz => ({ label: `${bz.bundesland} ${bz.bezirk_code}`, participants: bz.participants, reports: bz.reports }))
   616→    : stats.blStats.map(bl => ({ label: bl.bundesland, participants: bl.participants, reports: bl.reports }));
   617→
   618→  if (chartData.length > 0) {
   619→    renderBarChart(doc, chartData);
   620→    renderPieChart(doc, chartData.map(d => ({ label: d.label, value: d.reports })));
   621→  }
   622→
   623→  if (stats.bezirkStats.length > 0) {
   624→    doc.addPage();
   625→    renderBezirkTable(doc, stats.bezirkStats, stats);
   626→  } else if (stats.blStats.length > 0) {
   627→    doc.addPage();
   628→    renderBlTable(doc, stats.blStats, stats);
   629→  }
   630→
   631→  doc.addPage();
   632→  renderParticipants(doc, stats.participants);
   633→  renderFooter(doc);
   634→
   635→  doc.end();
   636→  return doc;
   637→}
   638→
   639→export function generateExercisePdf(
   640→  exercise: { name: string; date: string; exercise_type?: string | null },
   641→  stats: PdfStats,
   642→): PDFKit.PDFDocument {
   643→  const doc = new PDFDocument({
   644→    layout: 'landscape',
   645→    size: 'A4',
   646→    margins: { top: 30, bottom: 30, left: 40, right: 40 },
   647→    autoFirstPage: true,
   648→    bufferPages: true,
   649→  });
   650→
   651→  const formattedDate = new Date(exercise.date + 'T00:00:00').toLocaleDateString('de-AT', {
   652→    weekday: 'long', day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric',
   653→  });
   654→  renderHeader(doc, 'Auswertung', `${exercise.name} — ${formattedDate}`);
   655→  renderSummaryCards(doc, stats);
   656→  renderPerRepeater(doc, stats.perRepeater);
   657→
   658→  if (stats.blStats.length > 0) {
   659→    doc.addPage();
   660→    renderAustriaMap(doc, stats.blStats);
   661→  }
   662→
   663→  const chartData = stats.bezirkStats.length > 0
   664→    ? stats.bezirkStats.map(bz => ({ label: `${bz.bundesland} ${bz.bezirk_code}`, participants: bz.participants, reports: bz.reports }))
   665→    : stats.blStats.map(bl => ({ label: bl.bundesland, participants: bl.participants, reports: bl.reports }));
   666→
   667→  if (chartData.length > 0) {
   668→    renderBarChart(doc, chartData);
   669→    renderPieChart(doc, chartData.map(d => ({ label: d.label, value: d.reports })));
   670→  }
   671→
   672→  if (stats.bezirkStats.length > 0) {
   673→    doc.addPage();
   674→    renderBezirkTable(doc, stats.bezirkStats, stats);
   675→  } else if (stats.blStats.length > 0) {
   676→    doc.addPage();
   677→    renderBlTable(doc, stats.blStats, stats);
   678→  }
   679→
   680→  doc.addPage();
   681→  renderParticipants(doc, stats.participants);
   682→  renderFooter(doc);
   683→
   684→  doc.end();
   685→  return doc;
   686→}
   687→
